<tr><td>↳近 abase</td><td>/əˈbeɪs/</td><td>☆</td><td>★</td><td>动词</td><td>降低（地位、威望、尊严）</td><td>abase oneself before someone<br>在某人面前自贬</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 He would not abase himself by begging for the job.  他不愿低声下气地乞求这份工作。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 debase</td><td>/dɪˈbeɪs/</td><td>★</td><td>★</td><td>动词</td><td>贬低，贬损</td><td>debase the currency<br>使货币贬值</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Politicians who lie to win votes debase the office they hold.  为拉票而撒谎的政客贬损了他们所担任的职务。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 degrade</td><td>/dɪˈɡreɪd/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>动词</td><td>贬低，使降级；使退化</td><td>degrade the quality<br>降低质量</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Such insulting jokes degrade everyone who repeats them.  这类侮辱性的笑话贬低了每个转述它们的人。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 demean</td><td>/dɪˈmiːn/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>贬低，降低尊严</td><td>demean oneself<br>自贬身份</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 He refused to demean his colleagues in front of clients.  他拒绝在客户面前贬低同事。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 disgrace</td><td>/dɪsˈɡreɪs/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>及物动词、名词</td><td>使丢脸，使失去名誉；耻辱</td><td>disgrace the family name<br>使家族名声受辱</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The scandal disgraced a politician once admired by everyone.  这桩丑闻让一位曾受众人敬仰的政客名誉扫地。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 dishonor</td><td>/dɪsˈɑːnər/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★☆</td><td>及物动词、名词</td><td>使丢脸，玷污；不光彩</td><td>dishonor his memory<br>玷污他的名声</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Lying under oath would dishonor everything he had defended.  作伪证会玷污他一生所维护的一切。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 humiliate</td><td>/hjuːˈmɪlieɪt/</td><td>★★</td><td>☆</td><td>及物动词</td><td>羞辱，使丧失尊严</td><td>humiliate him in public<br>当众羞辱他</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The teacher never humiliated a student for asking a simple question.  这位老师从不因学生问简单的问题而羞辱他。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 shame</td><td>/ˈʃeɪm/</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>及物动词、名词</td><td>使羞愧，羞辱；羞耻</td><td>shame him into silence<br>让他羞愧得沉默</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The teacher never tried to shame students in front of the class.  这位老师从不在全班面前羞辱学生。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 sink</td><td>/ˈsɪŋk/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>动词、名词</td><td>下沉，下降；水槽</td><td>sink below the horizon<br>沉到地平线下</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The old boat began to sink soon after the storm.  暴风雨过后不久那条旧船开始下沉。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><del>↳近 smirch</del></td><td>/smɜrtʃ/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>及物动词、名词</td><td>玷污（名誉）</td><td>smirch someone's reputation<br>玷污某人名誉</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The rumor smirched the reputation he had built over thirty years.  谣言玷污了他三十年建立起来的名誉。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 take down</td><td>/teɪk ˈdaʊn/</td><td>★★★★★</td><td>★★★★</td><td>动词</td><td>贬低，使丢脸；记下</td><td>take someone down a peg<br>使某人收敛傲气</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The reporter tried to take him down in front of the whole crowd.  记者试图在众人面前贬低他。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 negate</td><td>/nɪˈɡeɪt/</td><td>★★</td><td>☆</td><td>及物动词</td><td>否认；否定，取消</td><td>negate the effect<br>抵消效果</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 One careless error can negate weeks of careful work.  一个粗心的错误能抹掉数周的细致工作。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 doubt</td><td>/ˈdaʊt/</td><td>★★★★</td><td>★★★★</td><td>名词、动词</td><td>怀疑，疑虑</td><td>cast doubt on<br>使人怀疑…</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 There is little doubt that the data was carelessly recorded.  几乎可以肯定，这些数据记录得很草率。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 distrust</td><td>/dɪsˈtrʌst/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>及物动词、名词</td><td>不信任，怀疑</td><td>deep distrust of authority<br>对权威的深深不信任</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Many villagers still distrust the promises made by developers.  许多村民仍不信任开发商的承诺。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 aggrandize</td><td>/əˈɡrændaɪz/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>及物动词</td><td>扩大（权力、财富、声望）</td><td>aggrandize one's power<br>扩张自己的权力</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The mayor used the crisis to aggrandize his own power.  市长利用危机扩大自己的权力。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 accept</td><td>/əkˈsept/</td><td>★★★★</td><td>★★★★</td><td>动词</td><td>接受，同意，承认</td><td>accept responsibility<br>承担责任</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 She accepted the award on behalf of her entire research team.  她代表整个研究团队接受了这个奖项。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 believe</td><td>/bɪˈliːv/</td><td>★★★★☆</td><td>★★★★☆</td><td>及物动词、不及物动词</td><td>相信，信任</td><td>believe her story<br>相信她的说法</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The jury seemed to believe every word of his testimony.  陪审团似乎相信他证词中的每一个字。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 credit</td><td>/ˈkredɪt/</td><td>★★★★</td><td>★★★★★</td><td>名词、及物动词</td><td>表扬，赞扬；信任</td><td>take credit for<br>把功劳归于自己</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 She deserves credit for keeping the project alive.  她值得称赞，因为她让这个项目坚持下来了。</td></tr>
</tbody>
</table>

## Unit 6

<table>
<thead><tr>
<th>单词</th>
<th>音标</th>
<th>常用</th>
<th>口语</th>
<th>词性</th>
<th>中文释义</th>
<th>常用短语</th>
</tr></thead>
<tbody>
<tr><td><b>discrepancy</b></td><td>/dɪsˈkrepənsi/</td><td>★★</td><td>★</td><td>名词</td><td>（在事实和宣称之间的）差异或矛盾</td><td>a discrepancy in the accounts<br>账目上的差异</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The auditors found a small discrepancy in the accounts of the charity.  审计人员在这家慈善机构的账目中发现了细小的差异。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 contrast</td><td>/ˈkɑːntræst/</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>名词、及物动词</td><td>差异，对比；对照</td><td>a sharp contrast<br>鲜明的差异</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 There is a sharp contrast between his words and his actions.  他的言语与行为之间有鲜明的差异。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 disagreement</td><td>/dɪsəˈɡriːmənt/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★☆</td><td>名词</td><td>分歧，不一致，争执</td><td>a sharp disagreement<br>激烈的分歧</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 A long disagreement over funding stalled the whole project.  关于资金的长期分歧使整个项目停滞。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 disparity</td><td>/dɪˈspærəti/</td><td>★★</td><td>☆</td><td>名词</td><td>悬殊差异，不一致</td><td>income disparity<br>收入差距</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The report highlights a growing disparity between the two regions.  报告强调两个地区之间日益扩大的差距。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 dissimilarity</td><td>/ˌdɪsɪməˈlærəti/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>名词</td><td>不同，差异</td><td>obvious dissimilarity<br>明显的差异</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Despite their shared origin, the dissimilarity of the two dialects is striking.  尽管同源，这两种方言的差异仍很惊人。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 distance</td><td>/ˈdɪstəns/</td><td>★★★★</td><td>★★☆</td><td>名词、及物动词</td><td>差距，距离；疏远</td><td>the distance between them<br>他们之间的差距</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 There is still a considerable distance between his theory and the observed facts.  他的理论与观察到的事实之间仍有相当大的差距。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 distinction</td><td>/dɪˈstɪŋkʃən/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>名词</td><td>殊荣，卓越；区别</td><td>a mark of distinction<br>卓越的标志</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 He graduated with distinction from a small rural college.  他以优异成绩从一所乡下的小型学院毕业。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 diversity</td><td>/daɪˈvɜːrsəti/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★☆</td><td>名词</td><td>多样性</td><td>a rich diversity of species<br>丰富的物种多样性</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The reef supports a rich diversity of species found nowhere else on earth.  这片珊瑚礁养育着地球上别处没有的丰富物种多样性。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 convergence</td><td>/kənˈvɜːrdʒəns/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>☆</td><td>名词</td><td>趋同，会聚</td><td>the convergence of views<br>观点的趋同</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Recent talks show a slow convergence of the two positions.  近期会谈显示双方立场在缓慢趋同。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 concord</td><td>/ˈkɑːŋkɔːrd/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★</td><td>名词</td><td>一致，和睦</td><td>live in concord<br>和睦相处</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The treaty brought decades of concord between the two states.  该条约给两国带来数十年的和睦。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 consonance</td><td>/ˈkɑːnsənəns/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>名词</td><td>一致，和谐</td><td>in consonance with<br>与…相符一致</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Her behavior was in consonance with her stated beliefs.  她的行为与她宣称的信念一致。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><b>discrete</b></td><td>/dɪˈskriːt/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>离散的，不连续的</td><td>discrete units of data<br>离散的数据单元</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The program stores each measurement as discrete units of data on separate lines.  程序把每次测量作为离散的数据单元分行存储。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 detached</td><td>/dɪˈtætʃt/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>冷漠超然的，不介入的</td><td>a detached attitude<br>超然冷漠的态度</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 She described the accident in a strangely detached voice.  她用一种异常冷漠的语气描述了那场事故。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 disconnected</td><td>/ˌdɪskəˈnektɪd/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>不连贯的，分离的</td><td>disconnected thoughts<br>不连贯的思绪</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 His speech was a series of disconnected anecdotes.  他的演讲只是一串不连贯的轶事。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 unattached</td><td>/ˌʌnəˈtætʃt/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>不相连的，独立的；未婚的</td><td>unattached to any group<br>不隶属任何团体</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The scholar remained unattached to any university for years.  这位学者多年来不隶属于任何大学。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 unconnected</td><td>/ˌʌnkəˈnektɪd/</td><td>★☆</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>不相连的，无关的</td><td>two unconnected events<br>两件互不相关的事</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Police believe the two robberies are entirely unconnected.  警方认为这两起抢劫毫无关联。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 separate</td><td>/ˈsepəreɪt/</td><td>★★★★</td><td>★★★★★</td><td>动词、形容词</td><td>分开，分离</td><td>separate the yolks<br>把蛋黄分离出来</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Please separate the recyclable waste from the ordinary rubbish.  请把可回收垃圾与普通垃圾分开。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 continuous</td><td>/kənˈtɪnjuːəs/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>连续的，不间断的</td><td>a continuous line<br>一条连续的线</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The machine has been in continuous operation for six days.  这台机器已连续运转六天。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><b>discretion</b></td><td>/dɪˈskreʃn/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★☆</td><td>名词</td><td>谨慎；自制；自行决定权</td><td>at your own discretion<br>由你自行决定</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 You may spend the remaining funds at your own discretion this quarter.  本季度剩余的资金你可自行决定如何使用。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><del>↳近 discreetness</del></td><td>/dɪˈskritnəs/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>名词</td><td>谨慎，慎重</td><td>the discreetness of her reply<br>她回答的谨慎</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 His discreetness about the deal earned everyone's trust.  他对这笔交易的谨慎赢得了大家的信任。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 prudence</td><td>/ˈpruːdəns/</td><td>★★</td><td>☆</td><td>名词</td><td>谨慎，审慎</td><td>financial prudence<br>财务上的审慎</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Prudence suggests we wait for the full report before deciding.  谨慎起见，我们应等完整报告出来再决定。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 continence</td><td>/ˈkɑntɪnəns/</td><td>★</td><td>☆</td><td>名词</td><td>自制，节制</td><td>practice continence<br>保持节制</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The monks were praised for their continence and quiet discipline.  僧侣们因节制与安静的自律而受称赞。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 discipline</td><td>/ˈdɪsəplən/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>名词、及物动词</td><td>自制，纪律；训练，学科</td><td>strict self discipline<br>严格的自律</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 It takes real discipline to practice the violin every morning.  每天早晨练琴需要真正的自律。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 inhibition</td><td>/ˌɪnhəˈbɪʃən/</td><td>★★</td><td>☆</td><td>名词</td><td>抑制；拘束感</td><td>lose all inhibition<br>全无拘束</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 After a drink he lost his usual inhibition and sang.  喝了一杯后他抛开平日的拘束唱起歌。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><del>↳近 refrainment</del></td><td>/rɪˈfreɪnmənt/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>名词</td><td>克制，忍住不做</td><td>refrainment from comment<br>对评论的克制</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 His refrainment from any complaint impressed the whole crew.  他对任何抱怨的克制让全体船员钦佩。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 restraint</td><td>/rɪˈstreɪnt/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>名词</td><td>克制，约束</td><td>show great restraint<br>表现出极大的克制</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 She showed remarkable restraint when the crowd booed her.  人群喝倒彩时她表现出惊人的克制。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 self-control</td><td>/ˌself kənˈtroʊl/</td><td>★★★★</td><td>★★☆</td><td>名词</td><td>自我控制，自制力</td><td>lose all self-control<br>完全失去自制</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 It took every bit of my self-control not to answer him back.  忍住不回击他用尽了我全部的自制力。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 self-restraint</td><td>/ˌsɛlf rɪˈstreɪnt/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★</td><td>名词</td><td>自我克制</td><td>exercise self-restraint<br>保持自我克制</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The officers exercised admirable self-restraint during the protest.  警员在抗议期间表现出令人赞赏的自我克制。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 imprudence</td><td>/ˌɪmˈpruːdəns/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>名词</td><td>轻率，不谨慎</td><td>an act of imprudence<br>一次轻率的行为</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 His imprudence with money ruined the small company.  他在金钱上的轻率毁了这家小公司。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 indiscretion</td><td>/ˌɪndɪsˈkreʃən/</td><td>★☆</td><td>☆</td><td>名词</td><td>不慎的言行，轻率</td><td>a youthful indiscretion<br>年轻时的轻率之举</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 One indiscretion in an interview cost him the appointment.  访谈中的一次失言让他失去了这个职位。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 incontinence</td><td>/ɪnˈkɑːntənəns/</td><td>★☆</td><td>☆</td><td>名词</td><td>不能自制，放纵</td><td>verbal incontinence<br>言语上的不加节制</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 His verbal incontinence at dinner embarrassed the whole family.  他晚餐时口无遮拦让全家尴尬。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><del>↳反 unconstraint</del></td><td>/ˌʌnkənˈstreɪnt/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>名词</td><td>无拘无束，不受约束</td><td>an air of unconstraint<br>一种无拘无束的气质</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The children played with cheerful unconstraint in the empty field.  孩子们在空地上无拘无束地欢快玩耍。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><b>discretionary</b></td><td>/dɪˈskreʃəneri/</td><td>★★</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>自主决定的</td><td>discretionary spending<br>自主决定的开支</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Managers have discretionary power over small purchases.  经理对小额采购有自主决定权。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 elective</td><td>/ɪˈlektɪv/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>形容词、名词</td><td>可自由选择的；选举的</td><td>an elective course<br>选修课程</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Most of the surgery performed here is elective, not urgent.  这里做的多数手术是可选择的，而非紧急的。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 voluntary</td><td>/ˈvɑːlənteri/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>自愿的，志愿的</td><td>voluntary participation<br>自愿参与</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Attendance at the evening seminar is entirely voluntary.  晚间研讨会完全自愿参加。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 compulsory</td><td>/kəmˈpʌlsəri/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★</td><td>形容词</td><td>强制的，必修的</td><td>compulsory education<br>义务教育</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Wearing a helmet is compulsory on every construction site.  在每个建筑工地戴安全帽都是强制的。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 mandatory</td><td>/ˈmændətɔːri/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>强制的</td><td>a mandatory requirement<br>强制性要求</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Safety training is mandatory for all new employees here.  这里所有新员工都必须接受安全培训。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><del>↳反 nonelective</del></td><td>/ˌnɑnɪˈlɛktɪv/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>非选择性的，必修的</td><td>nonelective surgery<br>非选择性手术</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The hospital continued nonelective procedures during the shortage.  医院在物资短缺期间继续进行非选择性手术。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><del>↳反 nonvoluntary</del></td><td>/ˌnɑnˈvɑləntɛri/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>非自愿的</td><td>nonvoluntary enrollment<br>非自愿的登记</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Critics called the program nonvoluntary in everything but name.  批评者称这个项目除名义外都是非自愿的。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 obligatory</td><td>/əˈblɪɡətɔːri/</td><td>★★</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>强制性的</td><td>obligatory military service<br>强制的兵役</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 In that country obligatory military service lasts for eighteen months.  在那个国家，强制的兵役要服十八个月。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 required</td><td>/rɪˈkwaɪərd/</td><td>★★★★</td><td>★★★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>必需的，规定的</td><td>required reading<br>必读材料</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 A signed form is required before the trip can begin.  出行前必须提交一份签好的表格。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><b>discriminate</b></td><td>/dɪˈskrɪmɪneɪt/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>☆</td><td>及物动词、不及物动词</td><td>区分；歧视</td><td>discriminate between the two<br>区分二者</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Young children can already discriminate between the two shades of blue.  幼儿已经能够区分这两种深浅不同的蓝色。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 differentiate</td><td>/ˌdɪfəˈrenʃieɪt/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>☆</td><td>及物动词、不及物动词</td><td>区分，区别；使不同</td><td>differentiate between species<br>区分不同物种</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 It is hard to differentiate the two brands by taste alone.  仅凭味道很难区分这两个品牌。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 discern</td><td>/dɪˈsɜːrn/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>动词</td><td>识别，辨别差异</td><td>discern a pattern<br>辨别出一种规律</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Even experts could barely discern a pattern in the scattered rainfall data.  就连专家也几乎辨不出这些零散降雨数据中的规律。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 distinguish</td><td>/dɪˈstɪŋɡwɪʃ/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★☆</td><td>及物动词、不及物动词</td><td>区分，辨别；使显著</td><td>distinguish right from wrong<br>分辨是非</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Young children cannot always distinguish fantasy from reality.  幼儿并不总能区分幻想与现实。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 prejudiced</td><td>/ˈpredʒədəst/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>有偏见的，成见的</td><td>prejudiced against outsiders<br>对外来者有偏见</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The judge dismissed the prejudiced juror before the trial began.  法官在审判开始前请走了那位有偏见的陪审员。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 biased</td><td>/ˈbaɪəst/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>有偏见的，偏向的</td><td>a biased report<br>有偏见的报道</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Readers complained that the coverage was clearly biased.  读者抱怨这些报道明显带有偏见。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 confuse</td><td>/kənˈfjuːz/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>及物动词</td><td>使困惑；混淆</td><td>confuse the issue<br>混淆问题</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The vague instructions confused nearly everyone in the training class.  含糊的说明让培训班几乎所有人都困惑。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 mistake</td><td>/mɪˈsteɪk/</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>名词、及物动词</td><td>错误，过失；误解</td><td>make a mistake<br>犯错误</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Everyone makes mistakes, but few admit them so quickly.  人人都会犯错，但很少有人这么快承认。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 mix up</td><td>/ˈmɪks ˈʌp/</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>★★★</td><td>动词</td><td>混淆，弄乱</td><td>mix up the names<br>把名字弄混</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 I always mix up the twins when they wear the same jacket.  这对双胞胎穿同样的外套时我总会认错。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 equitable</td><td>/ˈekwətəbəl/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>公平的，公正的</td><td>an equitable solution<br>公平的解决办法</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 They finally reached an equitable division of the property.  他们最终达成了对财产的公平分配。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 impartial</td><td>/ɪmˈpɑːrʃəl/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>公正的，不偏不倚的</td><td>an impartial observer<br>公正的观察者</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The dispute was settled by an impartial third party.  争端由一个公正的第三方解决。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 unbiased</td><td>/ʌnˈbaɪəst/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>无偏见的，公正的</td><td>an unbiased opinion<br>无偏见的意见</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 We need an unbiased assessment of the school's performance.  我们需要对这所学校表现的公正评估。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 impersonal</td><td>/ɪmˈpɜːrsənəl/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>客观的，不带个人感情的</td><td>an impersonal tone<br>不带感情的语气</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The report is written in a deliberately impersonal style.  这份报告刻意采用不带个人色彩的文体。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 unprejudiced</td><td>/ʌnˈprɛdʒədɪst/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>无偏见的，公正的</td><td>an unprejudiced view<br>无偏见的看法</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Only an unprejudiced reader can judge the argument fairly.  只有无偏见的读者才能公正评判这个论证。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><b>discursive</b></td><td>/dɪsˈkɜːrsɪv/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>（谈话内容）杂乱的</td><td>a discursive account<br>杂乱的叙述</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 His discursive account wandered from wine to politics and never reached a point.  他杂乱的叙述从红酒扯到政治，始终没讲到重点。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 desultory</td><td>/ˈdesəlˌtɔːri/</td><td>★</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>散漫的，无条理的</td><td>desultory conversation<br>散漫的谈话</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 They made desultory notes instead of a real research plan.  他们做了些散漫的笔记，而非真正的研究计划。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><del>↳近 digressional</del></td><td>/daɪˈɡrɛʃənəl/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>离题的，散漫的</td><td>a digressional lecture<br>离题的讲座</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 His digressional remarks made the lecture hard to follow.  他离题的评论让讲座难以跟上。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><del>↳近 excursive</del></td><td>/ɪkˈskɜːrsɪv/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>离题的；散漫的</td><td>an excursive narrative<br>散漫的叙述</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 His excursive lecture wandered far from the announced topic.  他的讲座散漫离题，远离了预告的主题。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 meandering</td><td>/miˈændərɪŋ/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>曲折的；散漫的</td><td>a meandering speech<br>散漫的讲话</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The meandering path followed every bend of the small stream.  这条曲折的小径顺着小溪的每个弯道。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 rambling</td><td>/ˈræmblɪŋ/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>漫无目的的，散漫的</td><td>a rambling explanation<br>散漫的解释</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 He gave a rambling answer that satisfied nobody present.  他给出散漫的回答，在场无人满意。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 wandering</td><td>/ˈwɑːndərɪŋ/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>游荡的；散漫的</td><td>a wandering discussion<br>散漫的讨论</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The wandering argument never reached any clear conclusion.  这番散漫的论述始终没有明确结论。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 concentrated</td><td>/ˈkɑːnsənˌtreɪtɪd/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>集中的，专注的；浓缩的</td><td>concentrated effort<br>集中的努力</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The team made a concentrated effort to finish before Friday.  团队集中努力想在周五前完工。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><b>disdain</b></td><td>/dɪsˈdeɪn/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>及物动词、名词</td><td>轻视，鄙视</td><td>disdain to answer<br>不屑于回答</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 She disdained the flattery of her ambitious colleagues.  她鄙视那些野心勃勃的同事的奉承。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><del>↳近 contemn</del></td><td>/kənˈtem/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>及物动词</td><td>轻视，蔑视</td><td>contemn the rules<br>蔑视规则</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 He seemed to contemn every tradition the school held dear.  他似乎蔑视学校珍视的每一项传统。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 disrespect</td><td>/ˌdɪsrɪˈspekt/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>名词、及物动词</td><td>轻蔑，不敬；不尊重</td><td>show disrespect for elders<br>对长辈不敬</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Interrupting her that way showed real disrespect.  那样打断她实在很不尊重人。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 slight</td><td>/slaɪt/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>形容词、及物动词</td><td>轻微的，不重要的；轻视，怠慢</td><td>slight her contribution<br>轻视她的贡献</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 He felt slighted when nobody mentioned his contribution during the ceremony.  典礼上无人提及他的贡献，他觉得受到了轻视。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 sniff</td><td>/ˈsnɪf/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>不及物动词、及物动词</td><td>嗤之以鼻，轻视；嗅</td><td>sniff at the offer<br>对这个提议嗤之以鼻</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Wealthy collectors sniffed at the young artist's early work.  富有的收藏家对这位年轻艺术家的早期作品嗤之以鼻。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 snub</td><td>/snʌb/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★☆</td><td>及物动词</td><td>轻视，不理睬</td><td>deliberately snub a colleague<br>故意冷落同事</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 She deliberately snubbed her colleague by ignoring him at the meeting.  她在会上故意不理睬同事，冷落了他。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 look down</td><td>/ˈlʊk ˈdaʊn/</td><td>★★★★★</td><td>★★★★☆</td><td>动词</td><td>轻视，看不起</td><td>look down on him<br>看不起他</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 She never looked down on people with less education.  她从不看不起受教育较少的人。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 honor</td><td>/ˈɑːnər/</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>及物动词、名词</td><td>尊敬，给以荣誉；荣誉</td><td>honor the veterans<br>表彰老兵</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The city honored the veterans with a quiet morning ceremony.  该市用一场安静的晨间仪式表彰老兵。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 respect</td><td>/rɪˈspekt/</td><td>★★★★</td><td>★★★★</td><td>名词、及物动词</td><td>尊敬，敬重；方面</td><td>show respect for<br>对……表示尊敬</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Students should learn to respect opinions that differ from their own.  学生应学会尊重与自己不同的意见。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><b>disengage</b></td><td>/ˌdɪsɪnˈɡeɪdʒ/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★☆</td><td>及物动词、不及物动词</td><td>分开，使脱离</td><td>disengage from the conflict<br>从冲突中脱离</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Both armies agreed to disengage from the conflict along the northern border.  双方军队同意从北部边境的冲突中脱离。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 disentangle</td><td>/ˌdɪsɪnˈtæŋɡəl/</td><td>★</td><td>☆</td><td>及物动词</td><td>解开，理清，使脱离</td><td>disentangle the wires<br>把电线理清</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 It took hours to disentangle fact from rumor in the report.  在这份报告中把事实与谣言分开花了好几个小时。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 untangle</td><td>/ʌnˈtæŋɡəl/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★☆</td><td>及物动词</td><td>解开纠结，理清</td><td>untangle the knot<br>解开这个结</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The auditors are still trying to untangle the company's finances.  审计人员仍在努力理清这家公司的财务。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 embroil</td><td>/emˈbrɔɪl/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>及物动词</td><td>使卷入纠纷，使陷入</td><td>embroil him in a lawsuit<br>使他陷入诉讼</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The memo embroiled the whole department in a bitter dispute.  那份备忘录把整个部门卷入了激烈的争执。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 entangle</td><td>/ɪnˈtæŋɡl/</td><td>★</td><td>★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>使缠住，使卷入；使复杂</td><td>entangle the negotiations<br>使谈判变复杂</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 New objections entangled the negotiations and delayed the deal for weeks.  新的反对意见使谈判陷入纠缠，把交易推迟了数周。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><b>disgorge</b></td><td>/dɪsˈɡɔːrdʒ/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>动词</td><td>排出，倾泻而出；呕吐出</td><td>disgorge its contents<br>吐出里面的东西</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The overturned truck disgorged its contents across three lanes of the highway.  翻倒的卡车把车上的东西吐了一地，横跨三条车道。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 belch</td><td>/ˈbeltʃ/</td><td>★</td><td>★</td><td>及物动词、不及物动词</td><td>喷出，冒出；打嗝</td><td>belch black smoke<br>冒出黑烟</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The old bus belched thick smoke as it climbed the hill.  那辆旧公交车爬坡时喷出浓烟。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 eject</td><td>/ɪˈdʒekt/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>喷出，排出；驱逐</td><td>eject the cartridge<br>弹出弹壳</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The machine ejected the disc with a loud click.  机器咔哒一声把光盘弹了出来。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><del>↳近 eruct</del></td><td>/ɪˈrʌkt/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>及物动词、不及物动词</td><td>喷出，喷发；打嗝</td><td>eruct steam and ash<br>喷出蒸汽与火山灰</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The volcano eructed ash over the sleeping villages below.  火山向下方沉睡的村庄喷出火山灰。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 expel</td><td>/ɪkˈspel/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>驱逐；开除</td><td>expel a student<br>开除一名学生</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The school expelled two students for cheating on the exam.  学校因考试作弊开除了两名学生。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 jet</td><td>/ˈdʒet/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>不及物动词、名词</td><td>喷射，喷出；喷气飞机</td><td>jet water into the air<br>把水喷向空中</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Hot water jetted from the crack in the old pipe.  热水从旧管道的裂缝中喷出。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 spew</td><td>/ˈspjuː/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★☆</td><td>及物动词、不及物动词</td><td>喷出，吐出</td><td>spew smoke and dust<br>喷出烟尘</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The chimney spewed gray smoke across the entire valley.  烟囱把灰烟喷向整个山谷。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 spout</td><td>/ˈspaʊt/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>及物动词、名词</td><td>喷出，涌出；喷口</td><td>spout hot water<br>喷出热水</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The whale surfaced and spouted a tall column of mist.  鲸鱼浮出水面，喷出一道高高的水雾。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 swallow</td><td>/ˈswɑːloʊ/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>及物动词、名词</td><td>吞下，咽下；燕子</td><td>swallow the pill<br>吞下药片</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 He swallowed the bitter medicine without a word of complaint.  他一言不发地咽下了苦药。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 ingest</td><td>/ɪnˈdʒest/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★☆</td><td>及物动词</td><td>摄入，咽下</td><td>ingest contaminated water<br>摄入受污染的水</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Villagers who ingest contaminated water often develop serious stomach illnesses.  摄入受污染水源的村民常患严重的胃病。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><del><b>disgruntle</b></del></td><td>/dɪsˈɡrʌntl/</td><td>☆</td><td>★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>使不满，使发怒</td><td>disgruntle the staff<br>使员工不满</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The sudden pay freeze disgruntled the entire department.  突然的薪资冻结让整个部门都不满。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><del>↳近 disaffect</del></td><td>/ˌdɪsəˈfekt/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>及物动词</td><td>使不满，使离心</td><td>disaffected voters<br>心生不满的选民</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The broken promises disaffected much of the party base.  这些空头承诺使党的许多基础支持者离心。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 displease</td><td>/dɪsˈpliːz/</td><td>★</td><td>★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>使不满，使不快</td><td>displease the audience<br>使观众不悦</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The change in schedule displeased nearly all the parents.  日程的变动让几乎所有家长不满。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><del>↳近 dissatisfy</del></td><td>/dɪˈsætəsˌfaɪ/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>及物动词</td><td>使不满，使不快</td><td>dissatisfy the customers<br>使顾客不满</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The vague reply seemed only to dissatisfy the audience further.  含糊的回答似乎只让听众更加不满。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 content</td><td>/ˈkɑːntent/</td><td>★★★★</td><td>★★★★</td><td>名词、及物动词</td><td>内容，内涵；使满足</td><td>the content of the letter<br>信的内容</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The teacher praised the style but questioned the content of my essay.  老师赞赏我文章的文风，却质疑其内容。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 gratify</td><td>/ˈɡrætɪfaɪ/</td><td>★</td><td>★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>使满足，使高兴</td><td>gratify a desire<br>满足某种欲望</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 It gratified her to see her students succeed.  看到学生成功让她很欣慰。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 please</td><td>/ˈpliːz/</td><td>★★★★☆</td><td>★★★★☆</td><td>及物动词、不及物动词</td><td>使高兴，使满意</td><td>please the crowd<br>取悦人群</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The simple ending pleased readers far more than critics expected.  这个简单的结局比评论家预料的更让读者满意。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 satisfy</td><td>/ˈsætɪsfaɪ/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>使满意；使确信</td><td>satisfy the skeptics<br>让怀疑者信服</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The extra evidence satisfied the skeptics who had doubted his story.  额外的证据让怀疑他说法的人信服了。</td></tr>
</tbody>
</table>

## Unit 7

<table>
<thead><tr>
<th>单词</th>
<th>音标</th>
<th>常用</th>
<th>口语</th>
<th>词性</th>
<th>中文释义</th>
<th>常用短语</th>
</tr></thead>
<tbody>
<tr><td><b>dignified</b></td><td>/ˈdɪɡnɪfaɪd/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>高贵庄严的，有威严的</td><td>a dignified silence<br>庄重的沉默</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The old woman answered the insult with a dignified nod and left.  老妇人以庄重的一点头回应了侮辱，随后离开。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 august</td><td>/ɔːˈɡʌst/</td><td>★★★★</td><td>★</td><td>形容词</td><td>威严的，庄重的</td><td>an august assembly<br>庄严的集会</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The august assembly of scholars rose when the old president entered.  当老校长进场时，这个威严的学者集会全体起立。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 distinguished</td><td>/dɪˈstɪŋɡwɪʃt/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>卓越的，杰出的；高贵的</td><td>a distinguished scholar<br>杰出的学者</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The prize went to a distinguished historian of medieval law.  奖项授予一位杰出的中世纪法律史学家。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 imposing</td><td>/ɪmˈpoʊzɪŋ/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>宏伟壮丽的</td><td>an imposing building<br>宏伟的建筑</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 An imposing stone building dominates the square at the city center.  一座气势宏伟的石砌建筑主宰着市中心的广场。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 solemn</td><td>/ˈsɑːləm/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>庄严的，郑重的</td><td>a solemn promise<br>郑重的承诺</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The ceremony was solemn and lasted almost two hours.  仪式庄严肃穆，持续了近两个小时。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 stately</td><td>/ˈsteɪtli/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>庄严的，堂皇的</td><td>a stately mansion<br>堂皇的宅邸</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 A row of stately elms lined the road to the house.  一排庄严的榆树沿着通往房子的路排开。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 flighty</td><td>/ˈflaɪti/</td><td>★</td><td>★</td><td>形容词</td><td>轻浮的，反复无常的</td><td>a flighty young man<br>轻浮的年轻人</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 She seemed flighty at first but proved remarkably reliable.  她起初显得轻浮，但事实证明非常可靠。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 frivolous</td><td>/ˈfrɪvələs/</td><td>★★</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>轻浮的；无关紧要的</td><td>a frivolous remark<br>轻率无谓的言论</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The judge dismissed the frivolous lawsuit within a single morning.  法官在一个上午之内就驳回了这起无理的诉讼。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 giddy</td><td>/ˈɡɪdi/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>轻率不严肃的</td><td>a giddy schoolgirl<br>轻率的女学生</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The giddy schoolgirls laughed through the entire serious lecture.  那些轻率的女学生在整场严肃的讲座中笑个不停。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 undignified</td><td>/ʌnˈdɪɡnɪfaɪd/</td><td>★</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>不庄重的，失体面的</td><td>an undignified scramble<br>不体面的争抢</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The ministers made an undignified rush for the exit.  部长们不体面地冲向出口。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><b>disinclination</b></td><td>/ˌdɪsˌɪnklɪˈneɪʃn/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>名词</td><td>不愿，无意</td><td>a disinclination to travel<br>不愿出行</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 His disinclination to speak in public cost him the promotion.  他不愿公开发言使他失去了升职机会。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 aversion</td><td>/əˈvɜːrʒən/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>名词</td><td>厌恶，反感</td><td>a strong aversion to<br>对…强烈厌恶</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 He has a lifelong aversion to crowded parties.  他一生都厌恶拥挤的聚会。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 disfavor</td><td>/dɪsˈfeɪvər/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>名词</td><td>不赞成；失宠</td><td>fall into disfavor<br>失宠，不受待见</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The old method gradually fell into disfavor among researchers.  这种旧方法逐渐不受研究者青睐。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 disliking</td><td>/dɪsˈlaɪkɪŋ/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★☆</td><td>名词</td><td>不喜欢，厌恶</td><td>a strong disliking for crowds<br>对人群的强烈厌恶</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 He made no secret of his disliking for formal dinners.  他毫不掩饰对正式晚宴的厌恶。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><del>↳近 disrelish</del></td><td>/dɪsˈrɛlɪʃ/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>名词、及物动词</td><td>厌恶，不喜欢</td><td>disrelish for the task<br>对这项任务的厌恶</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 She agreed to speak, though with obvious disrelish.  她同意发言，但显然很不情愿。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><del>↳近 mislike</del></td><td>/mɪsˈlaɪk/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>及物动词、名词</td><td>厌恶，不喜欢</td><td>mislike his manner<br>不喜欢他的举止</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The old captain misliked any change to the daily routine.  这位老船长厌恶日常安排的任何变动。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 unwillingness</td><td>/ʌnˈwɪlɪŋnəs/</td><td>★★</td><td>★</td><td>名词</td><td>不情愿，不愿意</td><td>unwillingness to compromise<br>不愿妥协</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Their unwillingness to answer questions worried the investors.  他们不愿回答问题让投资者担忧。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 appetite</td><td>/ˈæpəˌtaɪt/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>名词</td><td>欲望，爱好；食欲</td><td>an appetite for risk<br>对风险的偏好</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The public has a huge appetite for stories about lost cities.  公众对失落之城的故事有极大兴趣。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 favor</td><td>/ˈfeɪvər/</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>名词、及物动词</td><td>赞同，好评；恩惠</td><td>win public favor<br>赢得公众好评</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The proposal quickly won favor among the younger members.  这项提议很快赢得年轻成员的支持。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 fondness</td><td>/ˈfɑːndnəs/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>名词</td><td>喜爱，偏爱</td><td>a fondness for old films<br>对老电影的喜爱</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 He inherited his mother's fondness for long country walks.  他继承了母亲对乡间长途散步的喜爱。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 liking</td><td>/ˈlaɪkɪŋ/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>名词</td><td>喜爱，爱好</td><td>take a liking to her<br>对她产生好感</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The dog took an immediate liking to our new neighbor.  这只狗立刻就喜欢上了我们的新邻居。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 partiality</td><td>/ˌpɑːrʃiˈæləti/</td><td>★</td><td>☆</td><td>名词</td><td>偏爱；偏袒</td><td>a partiality for sweets<br>对甜食的偏爱</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The coach was accused of partiality toward his own students.  教练被指责偏袒自己的学生。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 preference</td><td>/ˈprefərəns/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★☆</td><td>名词</td><td>偏好，偏爱</td><td>a preference for quiet<br>偏好安静</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Most readers expressed a preference for the printed edition.  多数读者表示更偏好印刷版。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 relish</td><td>/ˈrelɪʃ/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>名词、及物动词</td><td>享受，喜爱；乐趣，兴味</td><td>with obvious relish<br>带着明显的享受</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 He described the meal with obvious relish.  他津津有味地描述那顿饭。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 inclination</td><td>/ˌɪnkləˈneɪʃən/</td><td>★★</td><td>☆</td><td>名词</td><td>倾向，意愿；倾斜</td><td>an inclination to agree<br>倾向于同意</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 She had no inclination to argue with him that evening.  那天晚上她无意与他争辩。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><b>disinfect</b></td><td>/ˌdɪsɪnˈfekt/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★☆</td><td>及物动词</td><td>消毒，使无菌</td><td>disinfect the wound<br>给伤口消毒</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Nurses disinfect the wound carefully before applying a fresh bandage each morning.  护士每天早上换新纱布前都仔细给伤口消毒。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 sterilize</td><td>/ˈsterəˌlaɪz/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★☆</td><td>及物动词</td><td>消毒，杀菌；使不育</td><td>sterilize the instruments<br>给器械消毒</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Nurses sterilize every instrument before the operation begins.  护士在手术开始前给每件器械消毒。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 infect</td><td>/ˌɪnˈfekt/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>感染，传染；影响</td><td>infect the water supply<br>污染供水</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Careless handling can easily infect the wound with bacteria.  处理不当很容易使伤口感染细菌。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 pollute</td><td>/pəˈluːt/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★★★★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>污染，弄脏</td><td>pollute the environment<br>污染环境</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Factories along the river have polluted the water for decades.  沿河的工厂几十年来一直在污染水体。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 contaminate</td><td>/kənˈtæmɪneɪt/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★★★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>污染，感染</td><td>contaminate the water supply<br>污染供水</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Leaking chemicals contaminated the soil around the factory.  泄漏的化学品污染了工厂周围的土壤。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 taint</td><td>/teɪnt/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>及物动词、名词</td><td>使（品质）污损</td><td>taint someone's reputation<br>损害某人名誉</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The scandal tainted his reputation for the rest of his career.  这场丑闻在他余下的职业生涯中都损害着他的名声。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><b>disjointed</b></td><td>/dɪsˈdʒɔɪntɪd/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>不连贯的，脱节的</td><td>a disjointed narrative<br>不连贯的叙述</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 His disjointed narrative jumped between years and confused everyone in the room.  他不连贯的叙述在不同年份间跳跃，把在场的人都搞糊涂了。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 disconnected</td><td>/ˌdɪskəˈnektɪd/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>不连贯的，分离的</td><td>disconnected thoughts<br>不连贯的思绪</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 His speech was a series of disconnected anecdotes.  他的演讲只是一串不连贯的轶事。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 unconnected</td><td>/ˌʌnkəˈnektɪd/</td><td>★☆</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>不相连的，无关的</td><td>two unconnected events<br>两件互不相关的事</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Police believe the two robberies are entirely unconnected.  警方认为这两起抢劫毫无关联。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 coherent</td><td>/koʊˈhɪrənt/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>连贯的，条理清晰的</td><td>a coherent argument<br>条理清晰的论证</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 He was too tired to give a coherent account of the accident.  他太累了，无法条理清晰地讲述事故。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 connected</td><td>/kəˈnektɪd/</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>连贯的，相连的</td><td>closely connected ideas<br>紧密相连的观点</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The two chapters are connected by a single recurring image.  这两章由一个反复出现的意象连接起来。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><del><b>disinter</b></del></td><td>/ˌdɪsɪnˈtɜːr/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>及物动词</td><td>（从墓地里）掘出</td><td>disinter the remains<br>掘出遗骸</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Archaeologists received permission to disinter the remains beneath the old chapel floor.  考古学家获准掘出旧礼拜堂地板下的遗骸。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><del>↳近 unbury</del></td><td>/ʌnˈbɛri/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>及物动词</td><td>掘出，挖出</td><td>unbury the coffin<br>挖出棺木</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Workers had to unbury the old pipeline before repairing it.  工人必须先挖出旧管道才能修理。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 unearth</td><td>/ʌnˈɜːrθ/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★☆</td><td>及物动词</td><td>挖出，发掘；发现</td><td>unearth ancient coins<br>挖出古代钱币</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Archaeologists unearthed a marble head near the temple wall.  考古学家在神庙墙边挖出一个大理石头像。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 bury</td><td>/ˈberi/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>埋葬，掩埋</td><td>bury the treasure<br>埋藏宝藏</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Squirrels bury nuts all over the garden every autumn.  松鼠每年秋天把坚果埋在花园各处。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 inter</td><td>/ɪnˈtɜːr/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>埋葬</td><td>inter the body<br>埋葬尸体</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The villagers gathered at dawn to inter the body beside the old chapel.  村民们黎明时聚集，把遗体埋葬在古老的小教堂旁。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 tomb</td><td>/ˈtuːm/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>名词</td><td>坟墓，墓穴</td><td>an ancient stone tomb<br>古代石墓</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The tomb had lain untouched for more than two thousand years.  这座墓穴两千多年来未被触动。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><del>↳反 inhume</del></td><td>/ɪnˈhjum/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>及物动词</td><td>埋葬，土葬</td><td>inhume the dead<br>埋葬死者</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The villagers inhumed their dead on the hillside above the river.  村民把亡者葬在河上方的山坡上。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><b>disinterested</b></td><td>/dɪsˈɪntrəstɪd/</td><td>★☆</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>公正的，无偏见的</td><td>a disinterested judgment<br>公正的判断</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 We need a disinterested judgment from someone with no stake in the outcome.  我们需要一个与结果无涉的人作出公正的判断。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 equal</td><td>/ˈiːkwəl/</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>相等的，同等的</td><td>equal pay for equal work<br>同工同酬</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The two shares are equal in value but not in voting power.  这两份股份价值相等，但表决权不同。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 evenhanded</td><td>/ˌiːvənˈhændɪd/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>公平的，不偏袒的</td><td>evenhanded treatment<br>公平的对待</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The referee was praised for her evenhanded handling of the match.  裁判因公平处理这场比赛而受到称赞。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 impartial</td><td>/ɪmˈpɑːrʃəl/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>公正的，不偏不倚的</td><td>an impartial observer<br>公正的观察者</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The dispute was settled by an impartial third party.  争端由一个公正的第三方解决。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 just</td><td>/ˈdʒʌst/</td><td>★★★★★</td><td>★★★★★</td><td>形容词、副词</td><td>公正的，公平的；仅仅</td><td>a just decision<br>公正的裁决</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Everyone agreed the settlement was a just outcome.  所有人都认为这项和解是公正的结果。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 nonpartisan</td><td>/ˌnɑːnˈpɑːrtəzən/</td><td>★☆</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>无党派的，不偏不倚的</td><td>a nonpartisan commission<br>无党派的委员会</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 A nonpartisan panel was asked to redraw the district lines.  一个无党派小组被要求重划选区界线。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 dispassionate</td><td>/dɪsˈpæʃənət/</td><td>★☆</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>冷静客观的，不带感情的</td><td>a dispassionate analysis<br>冷静客观的分析</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The judge gave a dispassionate summary of both arguments.  法官冷静客观地总结了双方的论点。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 objective</td><td>/əbˈdʒektɪv/</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>形容词、名词</td><td>客观的，公正的；目标</td><td>an objective assessment<br>客观的评估</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Reporters are expected to remain objective about the candidates.  记者应对候选人保持客观。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 square</td><td>/ˈskwer/</td><td>★★★★</td><td>★★★★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>贿赂，收买；使一致</td><td>square the local officials<br>把当地官员收买好</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The developer had squared the local officials long before construction.  开发商早在动工前就把当地官员收买好了。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 unbiased</td><td>/ʌnˈbaɪəst/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>无偏见的，公正的</td><td>an unbiased opinion<br>无偏见的意见</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 We need an unbiased assessment of the school's performance.  我们需要对这所学校表现的公正评估。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 unprejudiced</td><td>/ʌnˈprɛdʒədɪst/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>无偏见的，公正的</td><td>an unprejudiced view<br>无偏见的看法</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Only an unprejudiced reader can judge the argument fairly.  只有无偏见的读者才能公正评判这个论证。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 biased</td><td>/ˈbaɪəst/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>有偏见的，偏向的</td><td>a biased report<br>有偏见的报道</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Readers complained that the coverage was clearly biased.  读者抱怨这些报道明显带有偏见。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 inequitable</td><td>/ɪnˈekwətəbəl/</td><td>★</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>不公平的，不公正的</td><td>an inequitable distribution<br>不公平的分配</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Farmers called the water quotas deeply inequitable.  农民称这些用水配额极不公平。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><del>↳反 nonobjective</del></td><td>/ˌnɑnəbˈdʒɛktɪv/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>不客观的；非具象的</td><td>a nonobjective account<br>不客观的叙述</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Editors rejected the piece as a nonobjective attack on rivals.  编辑退了这篇稿子，认为它是对竞争对手的不客观攻击。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 one-sided</td><td>/ˌwʌnˈsaɪdɪd/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>片面的，偏向一方的</td><td>a one-sided report<br>片面的报道</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The debate felt one-sided because only supporters were invited.  这场辩论显得片面，因为只邀请了支持者。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 partial</td><td>/ˈpɑːrʃl/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>偏袒的，偏爱的；部分的，不完整的</td><td>partial to his friends<br>偏袒他的朋友</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The referee was clearly partial to the home team throughout the match.  整场比赛裁判明显偏袒主队。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 partisan</td><td>/ˈpɑːrtəzn/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>名词、形容词</td><td>党派偏见的；盲目拥护者</td><td>partisan politics<br>党派偏见的政治</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The debate quickly sank into partisan shouting about very old grievances.  辩论很快沦为带党派偏见的叫嚷，纠缠陈年旧怨。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 prejudiced</td><td>/ˈpredʒədəst/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>有偏见的，成见的</td><td>prejudiced against outsiders<br>对外来者有偏见</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The judge dismissed the prejudiced juror before the trial began.  法官在审判开始前请走了那位有偏见的陪审员。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 unjust</td><td>/ʌnˈdʒʌst/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>不公正的，不公平的</td><td>an unjust punishment<br>不公正的惩罚</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Many considered the sentence unjust and demanded a new trial.  许多人认为判决不公，要求重审。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><b>disjunctive</b></td><td>/dɪsˈdʒʌŋktɪv/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>分离的</td><td>a disjunctive structure<br>分离的结构</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The two clauses form a disjunctive structure that offers readers a clear choice.  这两个分句构成分离的结构，给读者一个明确的选择。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 discrete</td><td>/dɪˈskriːt/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>离散的，不连续的</td><td>discrete units of data<br>离散的数据单元</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The program stores each measurement as discrete units of data on separate lines.  程序把每次测量作为离散的数据单元分行存储。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 separate</td><td>/ˈsepəreɪt/</td><td>★★★★</td><td>★★★★★</td><td>动词、形容词</td><td>分开，分离</td><td>separate the yolks<br>把蛋黄分离出来</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Please separate the recyclable waste from the ordinary rubbish.  请把可回收垃圾与普通垃圾分开。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 disconnected</td><td>/ˌdɪskəˈnektɪd/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>不连贯的，分离的</td><td>disconnected thoughts<br>不连贯的思绪</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 His speech was a series of disconnected anecdotes.  他的演讲只是一串不连贯的轶事。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 disunited</td><td>/ˌdɪsjuˈnaɪtɪd/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>分裂的，不团结的</td><td>a disunited party<br>分裂的政党</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 A disunited opposition could not challenge the ruling coalition.  分裂的反对派无法挑战执政联盟。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 combined</td><td>/kəmˈbaɪnd/</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>联合的，共同的，合计的</td><td>combined efforts<br>共同的努力</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Their combined savings were still not enough for the house.  他们的存款合起来仍不够买房。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 connected</td><td>/kəˈnektɪd/</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>连贯的，相连的</td><td>closely connected ideas<br>紧密相连的观点</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The two chapters are connected by a single recurring image.  这两章由一个反复出现的意象连接起来。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 jointed</td><td>/ˈdʒɔɪntəd/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>有关节的，接合的</td><td>a jointed wooden doll<br>有关节的木偶</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The lamp has a jointed arm that folds against the wall.  这盏灯有一支可折向墙面的接合式支臂。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 mixed</td><td>/ˈmɪkst/</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>★★★★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>混合的，掺杂的；好坏交织的</td><td>mixed feelings<br>复杂的心情</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 I have mixed feelings about moving abroad.  对于出国我心情复杂。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 united</td><td>/juˈnaɪtɪd/</td><td>★★★★☆</td><td>★★★★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>联合的，团结一致的</td><td>a united front<br>统一战线</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The villages stayed united against the plan to divert the river.  各村庄团结一致反对改道河流的计划。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><b>dismantle</b></td><td>/dɪsˈmæntl/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>分解，分拆</td><td>dismantle the engine<br>拆解发动机</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Mechanics had to dismantle the engine completely to find the cracked valve.  机修工只得把发动机完全拆解，才找到那个开裂的气门。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 break down</td><td>/breɪk ˈdaʊn/</td><td>★★★★</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>动词</td><td>分解；故障，崩溃</td><td>break down into parts<br>分解成若干部分</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Bacteria break down the waste into simpler compounds.  细菌把废物分解成更简单的化合物。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 knock down</td><td>/ˈnɑk ˈdaʊn/</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>★★★</td><td>动词</td><td>拆卸；击倒</td><td>knock down the shed<br>拆掉棚屋</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 They knocked down the old barn and sold the timber.  他们拆掉了旧谷仓并卖掉了木料。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 assemble</td><td>/əˈsembəl/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>动词</td><td>聚集，集合；组装</td><td>assemble a crowd<br>聚集起人群</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Villagers assembled in the square to hear the announcement.  村民聚集在广场上听那则公告。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 construct</td><td>/kənˈstrʌkt/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>建造，建立；创造，想出</td><td>construct a theory<br>构建一套理论</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The lawyer constructed a careful argument from a handful of ordinary documents.  律师用少量普通文件构建出一套严密的论证。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><b>dismay</b></td><td>/dɪsˈmeɪ/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>使失去勇气；使不安，使焦虑</td><td>dismay his supporters<br>使支持者沮丧</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The abrupt policy reversal dismayed his supporters and delighted his rivals.  政策的突然反转让他的支持者沮丧，让对手高兴。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 chill</td><td>/ˈtʃɪl/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>及物动词、名词</td><td>使胆寒，使沮丧；寒冷</td><td>chill his enthusiasm<br>打消他的热情</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The news of the layoffs chilled everyone in the office.  裁员的消息让办公室里的每个人都心生寒意。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 daunt</td><td>/dɔːnt/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>及物动词</td><td>使胆怯，吓倒</td><td>daunt the bravest soldiers<br>吓倒最勇敢的士兵</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The steep climb ahead did not daunt the experienced mountain guide.  前方陡峭的攀登丝毫没有吓倒这位经验丰富的山地导游。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 dishearten</td><td>/dɪsˈhɑːrtn/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>及物动词</td><td>使沮丧，使失去信心</td><td>disheartened by the news<br>因这消息而沮丧</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The string of early losses disheartened even the most loyal fans.  开局连输让最忠实的球迷也失去了信心。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><del>↳近 dispirit</del></td><td>/dɪˈspɪrɪt/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>及物动词</td><td>使沮丧，使气馁</td><td>dispirit the whole team<br>使全队士气低落</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The endless rain dispirited even the most cheerful campers.  连绵的阴雨让最开朗的营员也沮丧起来。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 frustrate</td><td>/ˈfrʌstreɪt/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★★★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>阻挠，使受挫；使沮丧</td><td>frustrate one's plans<br>使计划受挫</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Heavy snow frustrated our plans to drive home.  大雪打乱了我们开车回家的计划。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 unnerve</td><td>/ʌnˈnɜːrv/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>及物动词</td><td>使紧张不安，使气馁</td><td>unnerve the young witness<br>使年轻证人紧张不安</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The sudden silence in the courtroom unnerved the young witness completely.  法庭上突然的寂静让年轻证人完全紧张起来。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 agitate</td><td>/ˈædʒɪteɪt/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★★</td><td>动词</td><td>煽动，激起；使不安</td><td>agitate for reform<br>鼓动改革</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Students agitated for changes to the exam system.  学生们为改革考试制度而奔走呼吁。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 bother</td><td>/ˈbɑːðər/</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>★★★★★</td><td>动词、名词</td><td>打扰，使烦恼；费心</td><td>don't bother me<br>别烦我</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The constant noise from the street really bothers me.  街上不停的噪音真让我烦。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 discomfort</td><td>/dɪsˈkʌmfərt/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★★</td><td>名词、及物动词</td><td>不适，不安；使不舒服</td><td>cause great discomfort<br>引起极大不适</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 He shifted in his seat with obvious discomfort.  他明显不自在地在座位上挪动。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><del>↳近 discompose</del></td><td>/ˌdɪskəmˈpoʊz/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>及物动词</td><td>使不安；使混乱</td><td>discompose the audience<br>让观众不安</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Nothing seemed to discompose the veteran pilot, not even engine failure.  似乎没什么能让这位老飞行员心慌，连引擎故障也不行。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 disquiet</td><td>/dɪsˈkwaɪət/</td><td>★</td><td>★</td><td>名词、及物动词</td><td>不安，忧虑；使不安</td><td>growing disquiet<br>日益增长的不安</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 There is growing disquiet among parents about the new curriculum.  家长中对新课程的不安情绪日益增长。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 distemper</td><td>/dɪsˈtempər/</td><td>★</td><td>★</td><td>及物动词、名词</td><td>使心烦意乱；（动物）瘟热</td><td>distemper his mood<br>搅乱他的情绪</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The long silence distempered everyone waiting in the hallway.  长久的沉默让走廊上等候的人都心烦意乱。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 distress</td><td>/dɪsˈtres/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>名词、及物动词</td><td>痛苦，忧伤；使痛苦</td><td>cause great distress<br>造成极大痛苦</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The sudden news distressed everyone in the small office.  突来的消息让小办公室里的每个人都很难受。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 perturb</td><td>/pərˈtɜːrb/</td><td>☆</td><td>★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>使不安，使烦扰</td><td>perturb the balance<br>扰乱平衡</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The sudden change of plan perturbed everyone on the team.  计划的突然改变让全队的人都心神不宁。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 unsettle</td><td>/ʌnˈsetəl/</td><td>★</td><td>★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>使不安，使动摇</td><td>unsettle the market<br>使市场动荡</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The sudden change of plan unsettled the younger students.  计划的突然变动让年幼的学生们不安。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 upset</td><td>/əpˈset/</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>及物动词、形容词</td><td>使心烦，使不安；打乱</td><td>upset the schedule<br>打乱日程</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The news of the closure upset the staff more than management expected.  停业的消息比管理层预想的更让员工不安。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 embolden</td><td>/ɪmˈboʊldən/</td><td>★</td><td>★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>鼓励，使大胆</td><td>embolden the rebels<br>壮了叛乱者的胆</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Early success emboldened her to ask for a promotion.  初期的成功让她有胆量要求升职。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 encourage</td><td>/ɪnˈkɜːrɪdʒ/</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>及物动词</td><td>鼓励，激励；促进</td><td>encourage students to read<br>鼓励学生阅读</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Good teachers encourage students to ask difficult questions.  好老师鼓励学生提出有难度的问题。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 hearten</td><td>/ˈhɑːrtn/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>及物动词</td><td>给予鼓励，鼓舞</td><td>hearten the troops<br>鼓舞部队士气</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The crowd's cheering heartened the runners on the last steep hill.  观众的欢呼在最后一个陡坡上鼓舞了跑者。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 nerve</td><td>/nɜːrv/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>名词</td><td>（坚强的）意志，勇气</td><td>lose one's nerve<br>失去勇气</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 He lost his nerve at the last moment and never asked the question.  他在最后一刻失去了勇气，始终没有问出那个问题。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 calm</td><td>/ˈkɑːm/</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>★★★★★</td><td>形容词、动词、名词</td><td>平静的；使镇静</td><td>calm down quickly<br>很快平静下来</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 She spoke in a calm voice until the children settled.  她用平静的声音说话，直到孩子们安静下来。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 compose</td><td>/kəmˈpoʊz/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★★</td><td>动词</td><td>使镇定；组成；创作</td><td>compose oneself<br>让自己镇定下来</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 He took a breath to compose himself before speaking.  他深吸一口气让自己镇定下来才开口。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 quiet</td><td>/ˈkwaɪət/</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>安静的；平静的</td><td>keep the room quiet<br>保持房间安静</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The library stays quiet even during exam week.  即使在考试周，图书馆也保持安静。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 soothe</td><td>/suːð/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>安慰，抚慰；减轻</td><td>soothe a crying baby<br>安抚哭闹的婴儿</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Soft music helped soothe the restless patient through the long night.  轻柔的音乐帮助安抚了焦躁的病人度过漫长的夜晚。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 tranquilize</td><td>/ˈtræŋkwəˌlaɪz/</td><td>☆</td><td>★</td><td>动词</td><td>使平静，给…服镇静剂</td><td>tranquilize the animal<br>给动物注射镇静剂</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The vet had to tranquilize the frightened horse.  兽医不得不给这匹惊慌的马注射镇静剂。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><b>disparage</b></td><td>/dɪˈspærɪdʒ/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>贬低；轻蔑地说</td><td>disparage someone's efforts<br>贬低某人的努力</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Do not disparage her work simply because she is young.  不要仅仅因为她年轻就贬低她的工作。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 belittle</td><td>/bɪˈlɪtəl/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★☆</td><td>及物动词</td><td>贬低，轻视</td><td>belittle her achievements<br>贬低她的成就</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Don't belittle her achievements just because she works quietly.  别因为她默默工作就贬低她的成就。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 denigrate</td><td>/ˈdenɪɡreɪt/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>诋毁，污蔑</td><td>denigrate a rival<br>诋毁对手</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 He never denigrates his opponents, even after losing badly.  即使惨败，他也从不诋毁对手。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 deprecate</td><td>/ˈdeprəkeɪt/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>及物动词</td><td>不赞成，反对；贬低</td><td>deprecate his efforts<br>贬低他的努力</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 He tends to deprecate his own achievements whenever anyone praises them.  每当有人称赞，他总倾向于贬低自己的成就。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 depreciate</td><td>/dɪˈpriːʃieɪt/</td><td>★</td><td>☆</td><td>及物动词</td><td>贬低…的价值</td><td>depreciate his contribution<br>贬低他的贡献</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Do not depreciate the value of patience in such long negotiations.  不要贬低耐心在这样漫长谈判中的价值。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 derogate</td><td>/ˈderəɡeɪt/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>动词</td><td>贬损，诋毁</td><td>derogate from an achievement<br>贬损某项成就</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Nothing should derogate from her remarkable achievement here.  什么都不应贬损她在这里的非凡成就。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 degrade</td><td>/dɪˈɡreɪd/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>动词</td><td>贬低，使降级；使退化</td><td>degrade the quality<br>降低质量</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Such insulting jokes degrade everyone who repeats them.  这类侮辱性的笑话贬低了每个转述它们的人。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 acclaim</td><td>/əˈkleɪm/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>名词、及物动词</td><td>称赞</td><td>win critical acclaim<br>赢得评论界赞誉</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Her first novel won wide critical acclaim across Europe and Asia.  她的第一部小说在欧洲和亚洲赢得了广泛的评论界赞誉。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 applaud</td><td>/əˈplɔːd/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>动词</td><td>称赞，赞成；鼓掌</td><td>applaud the decision<br>赞成这一决定</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Teachers applauded the decision to reduce homework for young pupils.  老师们赞成给低年级学生减少作业的决定。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 exalt</td><td>/ɪɡˈzɔːlt/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>赞扬；提升（地位）</td><td>exalt to the skies<br>推崇到极点</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Critics exalt her early novels as masterpieces of the era.  评论家将她早期的小说赞为那个时代的杰作。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 extol</td><td>/ɪkˈstoʊl/</td><td>★</td><td>★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>赞美；吹捧</td><td>extol his virtues<br>赞美他的品德</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Neighbors still extol his kindness during the long winter flood.  邻居们至今仍赞美他在漫长冬季洪水期间的善举。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 glorify</td><td>/ˈɡlɔːrəˌfaɪ/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>赞美，美化</td><td>glorify war and violence<br>美化战争与暴力</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Critics said the film glorified war instead of questioning it.  批评者说这部电影美化战争而非质疑它。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 laud</td><td>/ˈlɔːd/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★☆</td><td>及物动词</td><td>赞美，称赞</td><td>laud his courage<br>赞扬他的勇气</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Critics lauded the young pianist for her fearless interpretation.  评论家赞扬这位年轻钢琴家大胆的演绎。</td></tr>
</tbody>
</table>

## Unit 8

<table>
<thead><tr>
<th>单词</th>
<th>音标</th>
<th>常用</th>
<th>口语</th>
<th>词性</th>
<th>中文释义</th>
<th>常用短语</th>
</tr></thead>
<tbody>
<tr><td><b>disparate</b></td><td>/ˈdɪspərət/</td><td>★★</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>迥然不同的，全然不同的</td><td>disparate viewpoints<br>迥然不同的观点</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The committee somehow reconciled disparate viewpoints into a single workable proposal.  委员会竟把迥然不同的观点整合成一份可行的提案。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 dissimilar</td><td>/dɪˈsɪmələr/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>不同的，不相似的</td><td>wholly dissimilar styles<br>完全不同的风格</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The two novels treat love in dissimilar ways.  这两部小说以不同的方式处理爱情。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 distinct</td><td>/dɪˈstɪŋkt/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>清晰的，明显的</td><td>a distinct impression<br>清晰的印象</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 There is a distinct difference between the two dialects.  这两种方言之间有明显差别。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 distinguishable</td><td>/dɪˈstɪŋɡwɪʃəbəl/</td><td>★☆</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>可区分的，可辨别的</td><td>clearly distinguishable from others<br>与其他明显可区分</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The twins are barely distinguishable except for a small scar.  这对双胞胎除了一道小疤几乎无法区分。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 diverse</td><td>/daɪˈvɜːrs/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>不同的，多样的</td><td>diverse backgrounds<br>各不相同的背景</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The class brings together students from diverse cultural backgrounds.  这个班汇集了来自不同文化背景的学生。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><del>↳近 nonidentical</del></td><td>/ˌnɑnaɪˈdɛntɪkəl/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>不相同的</td><td>nonidentical twins<br>异卵双胞胎</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The two samples proved nonidentical under the microscope.  显微镜下这两个样本被证明并不相同。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><del>↳近 unalike</del></td><td>/ˌʌnəˈlaɪk/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>不相像的，不同的</td><td>utterly unalike in temper<br>性情上完全不像</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The brothers looked similar but were entirely unalike in temper.  兄弟俩外貌相似，但性情完全不同。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 alike</td><td>/əˈlaɪk/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>形容词、副词</td><td>相似的，同样地</td><td>look very much alike<br>看起来很相像</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The two paintings look alike until you study the brushwork.  这两幅画看起来相像，直到你细看笔法。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 identical</td><td>/aɪˈdentɪkəl/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>完全相同的，同一的</td><td>virtually identical results<br>几乎完全相同的结果</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The two samples produced identical readings on every machine.  两个样本在每台机器上都得出相同读数。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 indistinguishable</td><td>/ˌɪndɪˈstɪŋɡwɪʃəbəl/</td><td>★★</td><td>★</td><td>形容词</td><td>无法区分的，难以辨别的</td><td>indistinguishable from the original<br>与原件无法区分</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The copy was almost indistinguishable from the original painting.  这份复制品与原画几乎无法区分。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 kindred</td><td>/ˈkɪndrəd/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>同类的，相似的</td><td>a kindred spirit<br>志趣相投的人</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 We recognized each other as kindred spirits within the first five minutes.  头五分钟我们就认出彼此是志趣相投的人。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 like</td><td>/ˈlaɪk/</td><td>★★★★★</td><td>★★★★★</td><td>形容词、及物动词</td><td>相似的，像；喜欢</td><td>two of like mind<br>两个想法相似的人</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 They were of like mind on almost every important question.  他们在几乎所有重要问题上想法相似。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 parallel</td><td>/ˈperəˌlel/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>形容词、名词</td><td>相似的，对应的；平行的</td><td>a parallel case<br>类似的案例</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Historians found a parallel case in a much earlier war.  历史学家在一场更早的战争中找到了类似的例子。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 same</td><td>/ˈseɪm/</td><td>★★★★★</td><td>★★★★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>相同的，同样的</td><td>exactly the same size<br>完全相同的尺寸</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Both machines run at the same speed under normal load.  两台机器在正常负载下以相同速度运行。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 similar</td><td>/ˈsɪmələr/</td><td>★★★★</td><td>★★★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>相似的，类似的</td><td>similar in appearance<br>外观相似</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The two species are similar in size but differ in color.  这两个物种大小相似，但颜色不同。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><b>dispassionate</b></td><td>/dɪsˈpæʃənət/</td><td>★☆</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>冷静客观的，不带感情的</td><td>a dispassionate analysis<br>冷静客观的分析</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The judge gave a dispassionate summary of both arguments.  法官冷静客观地总结了双方的论点。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 disinterested</td><td>/dɪsˈɪntrəstɪd/</td><td>★☆</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>公正的，无偏见的</td><td>a disinterested judgment<br>公正的判断</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 We need a disinterested judgment from someone with no stake in the outcome.  我们需要一个与结果无涉的人作出公正的判断。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 equal</td><td>/ˈiːkwəl/</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>相等的，同等的</td><td>equal pay for equal work<br>同工同酬</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The two shares are equal in value but not in voting power.  这两份股份价值相等，但表决权不同。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 equitable</td><td>/ˈekwətəbəl/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>公平的，公正的</td><td>an equitable solution<br>公平的解决办法</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 They finally reached an equitable division of the property.  他们最终达成了对财产的公平分配。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 evenhanded</td><td>/ˌiːvənˈhændɪd/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>公平的，不偏袒的</td><td>evenhanded treatment<br>公平的对待</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The referee was praised for her evenhanded handling of the match.  裁判因公平处理这场比赛而受到称赞。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 impartial</td><td>/ɪmˈpɑːrʃəl/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>公正的，不偏不倚的</td><td>an impartial observer<br>公正的观察者</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The dispute was settled by an impartial third party.  争端由一个公正的第三方解决。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 just</td><td>/ˈdʒʌst/</td><td>★★★★★</td><td>★★★★★</td><td>形容词、副词</td><td>公正的，公平的；仅仅</td><td>a just decision<br>公正的裁决</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Everyone agreed the settlement was a just outcome.  所有人都认为这项和解是公正的结果。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 nonpartisan</td><td>/ˌnɑːnˈpɑːrtəzən/</td><td>★☆</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>无党派的，不偏不倚的</td><td>a nonpartisan commission<br>无党派的委员会</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 A nonpartisan panel was asked to redraw the district lines.  一个无党派小组被要求重划选区界线。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 objective</td><td>/əbˈdʒektɪv/</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>形容词、名词</td><td>客观的，公正的；目标</td><td>an objective assessment<br>客观的评估</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Reporters are expected to remain objective about the candidates.  记者应对候选人保持客观。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 unbiased</td><td>/ʌnˈbaɪəst/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>无偏见的，公正的</td><td>an unbiased opinion<br>无偏见的意见</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 We need an unbiased assessment of the school's performance.  我们需要对这所学校表现的公正评估。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 biased</td><td>/ˈbaɪəst/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>有偏见的，偏向的</td><td>a biased report<br>有偏见的报道</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Readers complained that the coverage was clearly biased.  读者抱怨这些报道明显带有偏见。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 inequitable</td><td>/ɪnˈekwətəbəl/</td><td>★</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>不公平的，不公正的</td><td>an inequitable distribution<br>不公平的分配</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Farmers called the water quotas deeply inequitable.  农民称这些用水配额极不公平。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><del>↳反 nonobjective</del></td><td>/ˌnɑnəbˈdʒɛktɪv/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>不客观的；非具象的</td><td>a nonobjective account<br>不客观的叙述</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Editors rejected the piece as a nonobjective attack on rivals.  编辑退了这篇稿子，认为它是对竞争对手的不客观攻击。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 one-sided</td><td>/ˌwʌnˈsaɪdɪd/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>片面的，偏向一方的</td><td>a one-sided report<br>片面的报道</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The debate felt one-sided because only supporters were invited.  这场辩论显得片面，因为只邀请了支持者。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 partial</td><td>/ˈpɑːrʃl/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>偏袒的，偏爱的；部分的，不完整的</td><td>partial to his friends<br>偏袒他的朋友</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The referee was clearly partial to the home team throughout the match.  整场比赛裁判明显偏袒主队。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 partisan</td><td>/ˈpɑːrtəzn/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>名词、形容词</td><td>党派偏见的；盲目拥护者</td><td>partisan politics<br>党派偏见的政治</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The debate quickly sank into partisan shouting about very old grievances.  辩论很快沦为带党派偏见的叫嚷，纠缠陈年旧怨。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 prejudiced</td><td>/ˈpredʒədəst/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>有偏见的，成见的</td><td>prejudiced against outsiders<br>对外来者有偏见</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The judge dismissed the prejudiced juror before the trial began.  法官在审判开始前请走了那位有偏见的陪审员。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 unjust</td><td>/ʌnˈdʒʌst/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>不公正的，不公平的</td><td>an unjust punishment<br>不公正的惩罚</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Many considered the sentence unjust and demanded a new trial.  许多人认为判决不公，要求重审。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><b>dispatch</b></td><td>/dɪˈspætʃ/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>名词、及物动词</td><td>迅速；发送，派遣</td><td>with great dispatch<br>极为迅速地</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The courier handled the urgent order with great dispatch and no complaints.  快递员极为迅速地处理了这份紧急订单，毫无怨言。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 alacrity</td><td>/əˈlækrəti/</td><td>★</td><td>☆</td><td>名词</td><td>欣然，反应迅速</td><td>accept with alacrity<br>欣然接受</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 He agreed to help with surprising alacrity and arrived within minutes.  他出人意料地爽快答应帮忙，几分钟内就到了。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 haste</td><td>/ˈheɪst/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>名词</td><td>急速，匆忙</td><td>in great haste<br>极为匆忙地</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 In her haste she left the tickets on the kitchen table.  她匆忙之中把票忘在了厨房桌上。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 swiftness</td><td>/ˈswɪftnəs/</td><td>★</td><td>★</td><td>名词</td><td>迅速，快速</td><td>the swiftness of the response<br>反应的迅速</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Everyone admired the swiftness of the rescue team's arrival.  所有人都赞叹救援队到达之迅速。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 expedition</td><td>/ˌekspəˈdɪʃən/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★☆</td><td>名词</td><td>迅速；远征，探险</td><td>handle it with expedition<br>迅速地处理它</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The clerk processed our papers with surprising expedition.  职员以惊人的速度处理了我们的文件。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><del>↳近 promptitude</del></td><td>/ˈprɑmptɪtud/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>名词</td><td>迅速，敏捷</td><td>act with promptitude<br>迅速行动</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 She answered every letter with unusual promptitude.  她以异常的迅速回复每一封信。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 transfer</td><td>/trænsˈfɜːr/</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>及物动词、名词</td><td>转移，调动；转让</td><td>transfer the funds<br>转移资金</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The bank will transfer the money to her account tomorrow.  银行明天会把钱转到她的账户。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 transmit</td><td>/trænzˈmɪt/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>及物动词</td><td>传送，发送；传播</td><td>transmit a signal<br>发送信号</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The station transmits its broadcast to villages across the valley.  该电台把节目发送到山谷各处的村庄。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 transport</td><td>/trænsˈpɔːrt/</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>及物动词、名词</td><td>运输，运送</td><td>transport the goods<br>运送货物</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Trucks transport fresh produce to the city every morning.  卡车每天早晨把新鲜农产品运往城市。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 pack off</td><td>/ˈpæk ˈɔf/</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>★★★</td><td>动词</td><td>打发走，送走</td><td>pack him off to school<br>把他送去学校</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 His parents packed him off to boarding school at twelve.  他十二岁时被父母送去了寄宿学校。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><del>↳反 leisureliness</del></td><td>/ˈliʒərlinəs/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>名词</td><td>从容，不慌不忙</td><td>the leisureliness of village life<br>乡村生活的从容</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 He worked with a leisureliness that infuriated his supervisor.  他工作时的悠然自得让主管十分恼火。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 delay</td><td>/dɪˈleɪ/</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>动词、名词</td><td>延迟，推迟</td><td>delay the departure<br>推迟出发</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Heavy fog delayed the flight by more than four hours.  大雾使航班延误了四个多小时。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 procrastination</td><td>/prəˌkræstəˈneɪʃən/</td><td>★☆</td><td>☆</td><td>名词</td><td>拖延，耽误</td><td>endless procrastination<br>无休止的拖延</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Months of procrastination left the report unfinished.  数月的拖延使报告仍未完成。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 accept</td><td>/əkˈsept/</td><td>★★★★</td><td>★★★★</td><td>动词</td><td>接受，同意，承认</td><td>accept responsibility<br>承担责任</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 She accepted the award on behalf of her entire research team.  她代表整个研究团队接受了这个奖项。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 receive</td><td>/rəˈsiːv/</td><td>★★★★</td><td>★★★★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>接收，收到</td><td>receive a package<br>收到一个包裹</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 She received the letter three weeks after it was posted.  这封信寄出三周后她才收到。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><b>dispose</b></td><td>/dɪˈspoʊz/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>及物动词、不及物动词</td><td>使倾向于；处理掉，丢弃</td><td>dispose of the waste<br>处理掉废弃物</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Volunteers helped dispose of the waste left behind after the summer festival.  志愿者帮忙处理掉夏日节庆后留下的废弃物。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 willing</td><td>/ˈwɪlɪŋ/</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>愿意的，乐意的</td><td>willing to help<br>愿意帮忙</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Several neighbors were willing to look after the children.  几位邻居都愿意照看孩子。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 inclined</td><td>/ˌɪnˈklaɪnd/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>倾向于的，愿意的</td><td>inclined to believe<br>倾向于相信</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 I am inclined to believe his version of the story.  我倾向于相信他讲的那个版本。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 predisposed</td><td>/ˌpriːdɪˈspoʊzd/</td><td>★☆</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>有倾向的，易于…的</td><td>predisposed to agree<br>倾向于同意</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Readers were already predisposed to accept his conclusions.  读者本就倾向于接受他的结论。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 prone</td><td>/ˈproʊn/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>倾向于…的，易于…的</td><td>prone to error<br>易出错的</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Handwritten records are prone to error and hard to search.  手写记录容易出错且难以检索。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 disinclined</td><td>/ˌdɪsɪnˈklaɪnd/</td><td>★</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>不愿的，不情愿的</td><td>disinclined to help<br>不愿帮忙</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 After the argument she was disinclined to help him again.  争吵之后她不愿再帮他。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 indisposed</td><td>/ˌɪndɪˈspoʊzd/</td><td>★</td><td>★</td><td>形容词</td><td>不愿的；身体不适的</td><td>indisposed to talk<br>不愿交谈</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The witness seemed indisposed to talk about that evening.  证人似乎不愿谈那晚的事。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><b>disregard</b></td><td>/ˌdɪsrɪˈɡɑːrd/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★★</td><td>及物动词、名词</td><td>漠视，不关注；缺乏兴趣，缺乏关心</td><td>total disregard for safety<br>完全不顾安全</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Their total disregard for safety rules eventually caused a serious accident.  他们完全不顾安全规定，最终造成了一起严重事故。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 ignore</td><td>/ˌɪɡˈnɔːr/</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>及物动词</td><td>忽视，不理</td><td>ignore the warning<br>忽视警告</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The captain ignored the warning and sailed into the storm.  船长忽视了警告，驶入了风暴之中。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 overlook</td><td>/ˈoʊvərˌlʊk/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>及物动词</td><td>忽视；宽恕；俯视</td><td>overlook a minor error<br>宽容小错</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The teacher chose to overlook the boy's late arrival.  老师决定不追究那男孩的迟到。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 slight</td><td>/slaɪt/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>形容词、及物动词</td><td>轻微的，不重要的；轻视，怠慢</td><td>slight her contribution<br>轻视她的贡献</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 He felt slighted when nobody mentioned his contribution during the ceremony.  典礼上无人提及他的贡献，他觉得受到了轻视。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 apathy</td><td>/ˈæpəθi/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>名词</td><td>缺乏兴趣，不关心</td><td>public apathy toward voting<br>公众对投票的冷漠</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Widespread apathy among young voters worried the campaign organizers.  年轻选民中普遍的冷漠让竞选组织者担忧。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><del>↳近 disinterestedness</del></td><td>/dɪsˈɪntrəstədnəs/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>名词</td><td>不感兴趣；无私公正</td><td>an air of disinterestedness<br>一副漠不关心的样子</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 His apparent disinterestedness hid a very strong personal ambition.  他表面的漠不关心掩藏着极强的个人野心。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
