<tr><td><del>↳近 incuriosity</del></td><td>/ˌɪnkjʊriˈɑːsəti/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>名词</td><td>缺乏好奇，不感兴趣</td><td>incuriosity about the world<br>对世界缺乏好奇</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Her incuriosity about other cultures surprised her traveling companions.  她对其他文化缺乏好奇，令旅伴们惊讶。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 nonchalance</td><td>/ˌnɑːnʃəˈlɑːns/</td><td>★</td><td>☆</td><td>名词</td><td>冷漠，漠不关心</td><td>studied nonchalance<br>刻意的淡然</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 She accepted the important award with surprising nonchalance.  她以出人意料的淡然接受了这个重要奖项。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 torpor</td><td>/ˈtɔːrpər/</td><td>★</td><td>★</td><td>名词</td><td>缺乏兴趣；（肉体或精神上的）迟钝，懒散</td><td>sink into torpor<br>陷入迟钝麻木</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The heat sank the whole village into a heavy afternoon torpor.  暑热让整个村子陷入沉沉的午后麻木。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><del>↳近 unconcern</del></td><td>/ˌʌnkənˈsɜːrn/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>名词</td><td>不关心，漠不关心</td><td>apparent unconcern for others<br>对他人明显的漠视</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 His unconcern about the deadline annoyed the whole team.  他对截止日期的漠不关心让整个团队恼火。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 heed</td><td>/ˈhiːd/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>及物动词、名词</td><td>注意，留心</td><td>heed the warning<br>留意警告</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Few sailors heeded the warning about the coming storm.  很少有水手留意关于风暴来临的警告。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 mind</td><td>/ˈmaɪnd/</td><td>★★★★☆</td><td>★★★★☆</td><td>及物动词、不及物动词</td><td>留意，注意；介意</td><td>mind the step<br>留意台阶</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Please mind the gap between the train and the platform.  请注意列车与站台之间的空隙。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 regard</td><td>/rɪˈɡɑːrd/</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>名词、及物动词</td><td>关注，重视；看待</td><td>have regard for others<br>顾及他人</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 He drives fast with little regard for other people.  他开车很快，几乎不顾及他人。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 attend to</td><td>/əˈtɛnd tu/</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>★★★</td><td>动词</td><td>留意，处理，照料</td><td>attend to the details<br>留意细节</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 A nurse stayed to attend to the patient through the night.  一位护士留下整夜照料这名病人。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 concern</td><td>/kənˈsɜːrn/</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>名词、及物动词</td><td>关心，担心；涉及</td><td>show genuine concern<br>表现出真切的关心</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Parents expressed concern about the safety of the playground.  家长们对操场的安全表示关心。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 interest</td><td>/ˈɪntrəst/</td><td>★★★★</td><td>★★★★</td><td>名词、及物动词</td><td>兴趣，关注；利息</td><td>show interest in music<br>对音乐表现出兴趣</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 She lost interest in the project after only a month.  仅一个月后她就对这个项目失去了兴趣。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><b>dissect</b></td><td>/dɪˈsekt/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★☆</td><td>及物动词</td><td>解剖；仔细分析</td><td>dissect the argument<br>仔细剖析该论点</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Critics dissected the argument line by line and exposed its hidden assumptions.  批评者逐句剖析该论点，揭出其隐含的假设。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 analyze</td><td>/ˈænəˌlaɪz/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>及物动词</td><td>分析，解析</td><td>analyze the results<br>分析结果</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The team spent weeks analyzing data from the survey.  团队花了数周分析调查数据。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><b>dissemble</b></td><td>/dɪˈsembl/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>动词</td><td>用假象隐藏真相，掩饰</td><td>dissemble his true motive<br>掩饰他的真实动机</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 He tried to dissemble his true motive, but his trembling hands betrayed him.  他试图掩饰真实动机，但颤抖的双手泄露了他。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><del>↳近 dissimulate</del></td><td>/dɪˈsɪmjəleɪt/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>及物动词、不及物动词</td><td>掩饰，假装</td><td>dissimulate his anger<br>掩饰他的愤怒</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 He tried to dissimulate his disappointment with a thin smile.  他试图用一丝浅笑掩饰失望。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 affect</td><td>/əˈfekt/</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>及物动词</td><td>假装，装作；影响</td><td>affect indifference<br>装作漠不关心</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 She affected surprise, though she had known for weeks.  她装作惊讶，其实早已知情数周。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 assume</td><td>/əˈsuːm/</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>★★★★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>就任，承担（职权）；假定</td><td>assume office<br>就职上任</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 She will assume office as director next Monday.  她将于下周一就任主任。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 bluff</td><td>/blʌf/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>动词、名词</td><td>虚张声势地骗人，唬人</td><td>bluff your way through<br>靠虚张声势混过去</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 He tried to bluff his way through the whole interview.  他试图靠虚张声势混过整场面试。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 counterfeit</td><td>/ˈkaʊntərfɪt/</td><td>★★</td><td>★</td><td>形容词、及物动词</td><td>仿制的，假冒的；仿制</td><td>counterfeit banknotes<br>伪造的钞票</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Police seized thousands of counterfeit banknotes hidden in the delivery van.  警方缴获了藏在送货车里的数千张假钞。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 fake</td><td>/ˈfeɪk/</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>形容词、名词、动词</td><td>假的，伪造的；伪造品</td><td>a fake diploma<br>假文凭</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The gallery unknowingly displayed a fake for almost ten years.  这家画廊不知情地展出了一件赝品近十年。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 sham</td><td>/ʃæm/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>动词、形容词</td><td>掩饰，假装；虚假的</td><td>a sham marriage<br>假婚姻</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The couple admitted their marriage was a sham arranged for the visa.  这对夫妻承认他们的婚姻是为签证安排的假婚姻。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 behave honestly</td><td>/bɪˈheɪv ˈɑnɪstli/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★☆</td><td>动词</td><td>诚实行事，不作伪</td><td>behave honestly with clients<br>对客户诚实行事</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 He promised to behave honestly even when no one was watching.  他承诺即使无人注视也诚实行事。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><b>disseminate</b></td><td>/dɪˈsemɪneɪt/</td><td>★★</td><td>☆</td><td>及物动词</td><td>传播，散布（信息）</td><td>disseminate new research<br>传播新的研究成果</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The society disseminated the findings to schools across the country.  学会把研究结果传播到全国各地的学校。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 broadcast</td><td>/ˈbrɔːdˌkæst/</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>及物动词、名词</td><td>广播，播送；散布</td><td>broadcast the news<br>播报消息</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The station broadcast the news of the rescue every hour.  电台每小时播报一次营救消息。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 circulate</td><td>/ˈsɜːrkjəˌleɪt/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>动词</td><td>散布，传播；流通</td><td>circulate a rumor<br>散播谣言</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Rumors of a merger circulated quickly among the staff.  合并的传言在员工中迅速传开。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 propagate</td><td>/ˈprɑːpəɡeɪt/</td><td>★★</td><td>☆</td><td>及物动词</td><td>传播，宣传</td><td>propagate new ideas<br>传播新思想</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Missionaries traveled far to propagate their beliefs among distant communities.  传教士远行到偏远社区去传播他们的信仰。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 gather</td><td>/ˈɡæðər/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>及物动词、不及物动词</td><td>收集，聚集</td><td>gather information<br>收集信息</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Volunteers gathered signatures outside the library all weekend.  志愿者整个周末在图书馆外收集签名。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 amass</td><td>/əˈmæs/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★☆</td><td>及物动词</td><td>积累，聚集（大量）</td><td>amass a fortune<br>积累财富</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Over thirty years she amassed an astonishing collection of maps.  三十多年间她积累了数量惊人的地图收藏。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 garner</td><td>/ˈɡɑːrnər/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>获得，积累</td><td>garner support<br>获得支持</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The proposal garnered support from both major parties.  该提案获得了两大党的支持。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><b>dissension</b></td><td>/dɪˈsenʃn/</td><td>★☆</td><td>☆</td><td>名词</td><td>意见分歧，不和</td><td>internal dissension<br>内部分歧</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Internal dissension over funding split the small research group into two camps.  关于经费的内部分歧把这个小研究组分成了两派。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 conflict</td><td>/ˈkɑːnflɪkt/</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>名词、动词</td><td>冲突，矛盾；抵触</td><td>a conflict of interest<br>利益冲突</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The judge withdrew because of a possible conflict of interest.  法官因可能存在利益冲突而回避。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><del>↳近 disaccord</del></td><td>/ˌdɪsəˈkɔrd/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>名词、不及物动词</td><td>不一致，不和</td><td>in disaccord with the plan<br>与计划不一致</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 His testimony was in clear disaccord with the written record.  他的证词与书面记录明显不一致。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 discordance</td><td>/dɪsˈkɔːrdəns/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>名词</td><td>不一致，不和</td><td>discordance between reports<br>各报告之间的矛盾</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 There is a clear discordance between the two witnesses' accounts.  两位证人的叙述之间存在明显不一致。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><del>↳近 discordancy</del></td><td>/dɪsˈkɔrdənsi/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>名词</td><td>不一致，不和谐</td><td>discordancy among members<br>成员之间的不和</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Growing discordancy among the founders slowed every decision.  创始人之间日增的不和让每个决定都拖延。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 disharmony</td><td>/dɪsˈhɑːrməni/</td><td>★</td><td>★</td><td>名词</td><td>不和谐，不一致</td><td>racial disharmony<br>种族间的不和</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Years of disharmony finally split the once famous band.  多年的不和最终使这支曾经著名的乐队解散。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 dissent</td><td>/dɪˈsent/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★</td><td>不及物动词、名词</td><td>持异议，不同意；反对正统</td><td>voice one's dissent<br>表达异议</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Two judges dissented from the majority opinion of the court.  两位法官对法院的多数意见持异议。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 dissidence</td><td>/ˈdɪsədəns/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>名词</td><td>异议，不同意</td><td>political dissidence<br>政治异见</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The regime punished even mild dissidence among its writers.  该政权连作家中温和的异见也要惩罚。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 dissonance</td><td>/ˈdɪsənəns/</td><td>★★</td><td>☆</td><td>名词</td><td>（音调）不和谐，刺耳；不一致，分歧</td><td>cognitive dissonance<br>认知失调</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Cognitive dissonance arises when our actions contradict the beliefs we hold.  当行为与所持信念矛盾时，认知失调便产生了。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 disunion</td><td>/dɪsˈjuːnjən/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>名词</td><td>分裂，不和</td><td>the threat of disunion<br>分裂的威胁</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The debate over slavery pushed the nation toward disunion.  关于奴隶制的争论把这个国家推向分裂。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 disunity</td><td>/dɪsˈjuːnəti/</td><td>★</td><td>☆</td><td>名词</td><td>不统一，不和</td><td>disunity within the party<br>党内的不团结</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Open disunity among the leaders cost them the election.  领导层公开的不团结使他们输掉了选举。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 division</td><td>/dɪˈvɪʒən/</td><td>★★★★</td><td>★★☆</td><td>名词</td><td>分歧，不和；分开，部门</td><td>deep division over policy<br>政策上的深刻分歧</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 There is deep division within the committee about the budget.  委员会内部在预算上存在深刻分歧。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 friction</td><td>/ˈfrɪkʃn/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★</td><td>名词</td><td>摩擦；冲突，不和</td><td>friction between them<br>他们之间的摩擦</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Constant friction between the two managers slowed the whole project.  两位经理之间不断的摩擦拖慢了整个项目。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><del>↳近 inharmony</del></td><td>/ɪnˈhɑːrməni/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>名词</td><td>不和谐，不协调</td><td>inharmony among members<br>成员之间不和</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Growing inharmony among members finally split the committee.  成员间日增的不和最终分裂了委员会。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 schism</td><td>/ˈskɪzəm/</td><td>★★</td><td>☆</td><td>名词</td><td>分裂，派系对立</td><td>a bitter schism<br>严重的分裂</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 A bitter schism split the party into two hostile factions.  一场激烈的分裂把该党撕成两个敌对派系。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 strife</td><td>/ˈstraɪf/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>名词</td><td>冲突，争斗，不和</td><td>civil strife<br>内乱</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Years of political strife left the country deeply exhausted.  多年的政治纷争使这个国家极度疲惫。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 row</td><td>/raʊ/</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>名词</td><td>争吵，争执</td><td>have a row with him<br>和他吵一架</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 She had a furious row with her landlord about the unpaid repairs.  她和房东就未付款的维修大吵了一架。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 divergence</td><td>/daɪˈvɜːrdʒəns/</td><td>★★</td><td>☆</td><td>名词</td><td>分歧，分离，背离</td><td>a sharp divergence of views<br>观点的明显分歧</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 A wide divergence of opinion delayed the final vote.  意见的巨大分歧推迟了最终投票。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 accord</td><td>/əˈkɔːrd/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★</td><td>名词、及物动词</td><td>一致，符合；授予</td><td>in accord with<br>与…一致</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 His actions were in accord with the policy he announced.  他的行动与他宣布的政策一致。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 agreement</td><td>/əˈɡriːmənt/</td><td>★★★★</td><td>★★★★</td><td>名词</td><td>一致，同意；协议</td><td>come to an agreement<br>达成协议</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The two firms signed an agreement to share research costs.  两家公司签署协议分担研究费用。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 concord</td><td>/ˈkɑːŋkɔːrd/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★</td><td>名词</td><td>一致，和睦</td><td>live in concord<br>和睦相处</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The treaty brought decades of concord between the two states.  该条约给两国带来数十年的和睦。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 concordance</td><td>/kənˈkɔrdəns/</td><td>★</td><td>☆</td><td>名词</td><td>一致，和谐；索引</td><td>in concordance with the data<br>与数据一致</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The results show close concordance with earlier field studies.  结果显示与早期实地研究高度一致。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 harmony</td><td>/ˈhɑːrməni/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★★</td><td>名词</td><td>和谐，融洽</td><td>live in harmony with<br>与…和谐共处</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The two communities have lived in harmony for generations.  这两个社区世代和睦相处。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 consensus</td><td>/kənˈsensəs/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>名词</td><td>一致同意</td><td>reach a consensus<br>达成一致意见</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The board finally reached a consensus after four hours of debate.  董事会经过四小时辩论终于达成了一致。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 unanimity</td><td>/ˌjuːnəˈnɪməti/</td><td>★☆</td><td>☆</td><td>名词</td><td>全体一致，一致同意</td><td>rare unanimity<br>难得的一致</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 There was surprising unanimity among the usually divided members.  在平时意见分歧的成员中出现了惊人的一致。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><b>dissent</b></td><td>/dɪˈsent/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★</td><td>不及物动词、名词</td><td>持异议，不同意；反对正统</td><td>voice one's dissent<br>表达异议</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Two judges dissented from the majority opinion of the court.  两位法官对法院的多数意见持异议。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 differ</td><td>/ˈdɪfər/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>不及物动词</td><td>不同；意见不一致</td><td>differ in opinion<br>意见不同</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Experts differ widely on how the disease should be treated.  专家们对该疾病的治疗方式看法大相径庭。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 disagree</td><td>/dɪsəˈɡriː/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>不及物动词</td><td>不同意，有分歧</td><td>disagree with the plan<br>不同意这个计划</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Two experts openly disagreed about the cause of the accident.  两位专家对事故原因公开表示分歧。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><del>↳近 nonconcur</del></td><td>/ˌnɑnkənˈkɜr/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>不及物动词</td><td>不同意，不赞同</td><td>nonconcur with the finding<br>不同意该结论</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Two members formally nonconcurred with the committee's recommendation.  两名成员正式表示不同意委员会的建议。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 heterodoxy</td><td>/ˈhetərədɑːksi/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>名词</td><td>异端邪说；非正统观点</td><td>charged with heterodoxy<br>被指为异端</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The bishop condemned her writings as dangerous heterodoxy.  主教谴责她的著作是危险的异端邪说。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 nonconformity</td><td>/ˌnɑːnkənˈfɔːrməti/</td><td>★</td><td>☆</td><td>名词</td><td>不墨守成规，不遵从</td><td>a spirit of nonconformity<br>不循规蹈矩的精神</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Her nonconformity made her unpopular with the school administration.  她的不合常规使她不受校方欢迎。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 agree</td><td>/əˈɡriː/</td><td>★★★★</td><td>★★★★</td><td>动词</td><td>同意，赞同；一致</td><td>agree to the terms<br>同意这些条款</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Both sides finally agreed to the new safety rules.  双方最终同意了新的安全规定。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 assent</td><td>/əˈsent/</td><td>★★</td><td>★</td><td>不及物动词、名词</td><td>同意，赞同</td><td>assent to the proposal<br>赞同该提议</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The committee assented to the proposal without further debate.  委员会未经进一步辩论就同意了该提案。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 concur</td><td>/kənˈkɜːr/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>不及物动词</td><td>同意，一致；同时发生</td><td>concur with the judge<br>同意法官的意见</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Two other judges concurred with the opinion she had written.  另外两位法官同意她撰写的意见。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 conformity</td><td>/kənˈfɔːrməti/</td><td>★★</td><td>☆</td><td>名词</td><td>遵从，一致，顺从</td><td>conformity to social norms<br>遵从社会规范</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The company demands strict conformity to its dress code.  公司要求严格遵守其着装规范。</td></tr>
</tbody>
</table>

## Unit 9

<table>
<thead><tr>
<th>单词</th>
<th>音标</th>
<th>常用</th>
<th>口语</th>
<th>词性</th>
<th>中文释义</th>
<th>常用短语</th>
</tr></thead>
<tbody>
<tr><td><b>dissipate</b></td><td>/ˈdɪsɪpeɪt/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★</td><td>及物动词、不及物动词</td><td>驱散，消散；浪费</td><td>dissipate the fog<br>驱散雾气</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The morning mist dissipated as soon as the sun rose.  太阳一升起，晨雾就消散了。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 disband</td><td>/dɪsˈbænd/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★☆</td><td>动词</td><td>解散，遣散</td><td>disband the committee<br>解散委员会</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The band disbanded after their final concert last year.  乐队在去年最后一场演唱会后解散。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 dispel</td><td>/dɪˈspel/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>驱散，消除（疑虑）</td><td>dispel the rumors<br>消除谣言</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Clear figures from the auditor dispelled most of the doubts.  审计师提供的清晰数据消除了大部分疑虑。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 disperse</td><td>/dɪˈspɜːrs/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>动词</td><td>分散，散开，驱散</td><td>disperse the crowd<br>驱散人群</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Police asked the protesters to disperse before nightfall.  警方要求抗议者在天黑前散开。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 scatter</td><td>/ˈskætər/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>动词</td><td>散开，四散；撒播</td><td>scatter the seeds<br>把种子撒开</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The birds scattered the moment the gardener opened the gate.  园丁一开门，鸟就四散飞走了。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 waste</td><td>/ˈweɪst/</td><td>★★★★</td><td>★★★★</td><td>形容词、名词、动词</td><td>荒芜的；浪费；废物</td><td>waste land<br>荒地</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Nothing grows on this waste ground beside the old factory.  旧工厂旁的这片荒地上什么也长不出来。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 squander</td><td>/ˈskwɑːndər/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★☆</td><td>及物动词</td><td>浪费</td><td>squander an opportunity<br>浪费机会</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Do not squander this opportunity to study abroad for a year.  不要浪费这次出国留学一年的机会。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 lavish</td><td>/ˈlævɪʃ/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>形容词、及物动词</td><td>奢侈的，大量的，无节制的；挥霍，浪费</td><td>a lavish banquet<br>奢华的宴会</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 They hosted a lavish banquet for three hundred guests in the garden.  他们在花园里为三百位客人举办了一场奢华的宴会。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 accumulate</td><td>/əˈkjuːmjəleɪt/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★★</td><td>不及物动词、及物动词</td><td>逐渐增长，积累</td><td>accumulate wealth<br>积累财富</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Dust had accumulated on the shelves during our absence.  我们离开期间架子上积了灰。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 gather</td><td>/ˈɡæðər/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>及物动词、不及物动词</td><td>收集，聚集</td><td>gather information<br>收集信息</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Volunteers gathered signatures outside the library all weekend.  志愿者整个周末在图书馆外收集签名。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 amass</td><td>/əˈmæs/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★☆</td><td>及物动词</td><td>积累，聚集（大量）</td><td>amass a fortune<br>积累财富</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Over thirty years she amassed an astonishing collection of maps.  三十多年间她积累了数量惊人的地图收藏。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 cluster</td><td>/ˈklʌstər/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>名词</td><td>一群，一簇</td><td>a cluster of houses<br>一簇房屋</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 A small cluster of reporters waited outside the courthouse door.  一小群记者等在法院门口。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 assemble</td><td>/əˈsembəl/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>动词</td><td>聚集，集合；组装</td><td>assemble a crowd<br>聚集起人群</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Villagers assembled in the square to hear the announcement.  村民聚集在广场上听那则公告。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 concentrate</td><td>/ˈkɑːnsntreɪt/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>动词</td><td>浓缩；聚集</td><td>concentrate the solution<br>浓缩溶液</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Boil the syrup slowly in order to concentrate the solution.  慢慢熬煮糖浆以便浓缩溶液。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 congregate</td><td>/ˈkɑːŋɡrəˌɡeɪt/</td><td>★☆</td><td>☆</td><td>动词</td><td>聚集，集合</td><td>congregate in the hall<br>聚集在大厅里</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Students congregate near the fountain between morning classes.  上午课间学生们聚集在喷泉附近。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 conserve</td><td>/kənˈsɜːrv/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>节约，保存，保护</td><td>conserve energy<br>节约能源</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 We must conserve water during the long summer drought.  漫长的夏季干旱期间我们必须节约用水。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><b>dissolute</b></td><td>/ˈdɪsəluːt/</td><td>★</td><td>★</td><td>形容词</td><td>放荡的，无节制的</td><td>a dissolute lifestyle<br>放荡的生活方式</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The novel follows a dissolute lifestyle of late nights and squandered fortunes.  小说描写了通宵纵乐、挥霍家财的放荡生活方式。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 dissipated</td><td>/ˈdɪsəˌpeɪtɪd/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>放荡的，沉溺酒色的</td><td>a dissipated young aristocrat<br>放荡的年轻贵族</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Years of dissipated living had ruined his health completely.  多年放荡的生活彻底毁了他的健康。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 libertine</td><td>/ˈlɪbərtiːn/</td><td>★</td><td>★</td><td>名词</td><td>放荡不羁者</td><td>an unrepentant libertine<br>不知悔改的放荡者</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The play portrays an unrepentant libertine who ruins everyone around him.  这部戏刻画了一个毁掉身边所有人的不知悔改的放荡者。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 rakish</td><td>/ˈreɪkɪʃ/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>放荡的，行为不检点的</td><td>a rakish reputation<br>放荡不检的名声</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 His rakish reputation kept respectable families from inviting him to dinner.  他放荡不检的名声使正派家庭不愿请他吃饭。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 reprobate</td><td>/ˈreprəbeɪt/</td><td>★</td><td>☆</td><td>名词</td><td>堕落者，道德败坏的人</td><td>an old reprobate<br>一个老无赖</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The novel's hero is a charming reprobate with no money.  小说的主角是个迷人却身无分文的堕落者。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 abstinent</td><td>/ˈæbstənənt/</td><td>★</td><td>★</td><td>形容词</td><td>节制的，禁欲的</td><td>remain abstinent from alcohol<br>戒酒不饮</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 He stayed abstinent from meat and wine for ten years.  他十年间不吃肉不喝酒。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 abstemious</td><td>/əbˈstiːmiəs/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>（吃喝等）有节制的，节俭的</td><td>an abstemious diet<br>有节制的饮食</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 He led an abstemious life, eating little and drinking less.  他生活节制，吃得少喝得更少。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 temperate</td><td>/ˈtempərət/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>（言行举止）有分寸的，有节制的</td><td>a temperate response<br>有分寸的回应</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 She gave a temperate reply to a very rude question.  面对无礼的问题她的回答很有分寸。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 ascetic</td><td>/əˈsetɪk/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★☆</td><td>名词、形容词</td><td>苦行的，禁欲的；苦行者</td><td>an ascetic lifestyle<br>苦修的生活方式</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The monk led an ascetic life on bread and cold water.  这位僧人以面包和冷水过着自我克制的生活。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 stoic</td><td>/ˈstoʊɪk/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>隐忍的，冷静的</td><td>stoic acceptance of pain<br>隐忍地承受痛苦</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Her stoic acceptance of pain impressed even the veteran nurses.  她隐忍地承受痛苦，连资深护士都为之动容。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 spartan</td><td>/ˈspɑːrtn/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>简朴的，节约的</td><td>spartan living conditions<br>简朴的生活条件</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The monks lived in spartan rooms with only a bed and a lamp.  僧侣们住在只有一床一灯的简朴房间里。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 self-denying</td><td>/ˌself dɪˈnaɪɪŋ/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★</td><td>形容词</td><td>克己的，自我节制的</td><td>a self-denying habit<br>克己的习惯</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 His self-denying routine allowed no sweets at all.  他克己的作息完全不允许甜食。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 uncorrupted</td><td>/ˌʌnkəˈrʌptɪd/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>未腐化的，纯洁的</td><td>uncorrupted by power<br>未被权力腐蚀</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 He remained uncorrupted despite decades in machine politics.  尽管在政治机器中浸泡数十年，他仍未被腐蚀。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><b>dissolve</b></td><td>/dɪˈzɑːlv/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>动词</td><td>溶解，融化；解散</td><td>dissolve in water<br>在水中溶解</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Stir the powder until the crystals dissolve in water completely.  搅拌粉末，直到晶体在水中完全溶解。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 liquefy</td><td>/ˈlɪkwəˌfaɪ/</td><td>★</td><td>★</td><td>动词</td><td>使液化，变成液体</td><td>liquefy the mixture<br>使混合物液化</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Heat will liquefy the butter in about two minutes.  加热约两分钟就能把黄油化成液体。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 melt</td><td>/ˈmelt/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>动词</td><td>融化，熔化</td><td>melt in the sun<br>在阳光下融化</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The ice cream melted before we reached the beach.  我们还没到海滩冰淇淋就化了。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 disintegrate</td><td>/dɪsˈɪntɪɡreɪt/</td><td>★☆</td><td>☆</td><td>动词</td><td>分解，碎裂；瓦解</td><td>disintegrate into fragments<br>碎裂成片</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The old manuscript began to disintegrate the moment we touched it.  我们一碰那份古老手稿，它就开始碎裂。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 disband</td><td>/dɪsˈbænd/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★☆</td><td>动词</td><td>解散，遣散</td><td>disband the committee<br>解散委员会</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The band disbanded after their final concert last year.  乐队在去年最后一场演唱会后解散。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 break up</td><td>/breɪk ʌp/</td><td>★★★★</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>动词</td><td>分开，分裂；解散</td><td>break up the group<br>拆散这个团体</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Police arrived quickly to break up the noisy crowd.  警察迅速赶到驱散嘈杂的人群。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 coagulate</td><td>/koʊˈæɡjuleɪt/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>动词</td><td>凝结，凝固</td><td>coagulate into clots<br>凝结成块</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Blood from the small cut coagulated within a few minutes.  小伤口流出的血几分钟内就凝结了。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 solidify</td><td>/səˈlɪdəˌfaɪ/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>动词</td><td>使凝固；使稳固</td><td>solidify into a mass<br>凝固成一块</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The melted wax solidified quickly in the cold room.  融化的蜡在冷房间里很快凝固了。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 freeze</td><td>/ˈfriːz/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>动词、名词</td><td>结冰，冻住；冻结</td><td>freeze solid<br>冻得结实</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The pond will freeze if the temperature drops below zero tonight.  如果今晚气温降到零度以下，池塘会结冰。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 consolidate</td><td>/kənˈsɑːlɪdeɪt/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>☆</td><td>动词</td><td>巩固，加强；合并</td><td>consolidate their power<br>巩固他们的权力</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The new king moved quickly to consolidate his hold over the northern provinces.  新国王迅速行动，巩固他对北方各省的控制。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 unify</td><td>/ˈjuːnəˌfaɪ/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>统一，使成一体</td><td>unify the country<br>统一国家</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 A common language helped unify the scattered tribes into one nation.  共同的语言帮助把分散的部落统一为一个民族。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><b>dissonance</b></td><td>/ˈdɪsənəns/</td><td>★★</td><td>☆</td><td>名词</td><td>（音调）不和谐，刺耳；不一致，分歧</td><td>cognitive dissonance<br>认知失调</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Cognitive dissonance arises when our actions contradict the beliefs we hold.  当行为与所持信念矛盾时，认知失调便产生了。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 conflict</td><td>/ˈkɑːnflɪkt/</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>名词、动词</td><td>冲突，矛盾；抵触</td><td>a conflict of interest<br>利益冲突</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The judge withdrew because of a possible conflict of interest.  法官因可能存在利益冲突而回避。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><del>↳近 disaccord</del></td><td>/ˌdɪsəˈkɔrd/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>名词、不及物动词</td><td>不一致，不和</td><td>in disaccord with the plan<br>与计划不一致</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 His testimony was in clear disaccord with the written record.  他的证词与书面记录明显不一致。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 discordance</td><td>/dɪsˈkɔːrdəns/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>名词</td><td>不一致，不和</td><td>discordance between reports<br>各报告之间的矛盾</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 There is a clear discordance between the two witnesses' accounts.  两位证人的叙述之间存在明显不一致。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><del>↳近 discordancy</del></td><td>/dɪsˈkɔrdənsi/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>名词</td><td>不一致，不和谐</td><td>discordancy among members<br>成员之间的不和</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Growing discordancy among the founders slowed every decision.  创始人之间日增的不和让每个决定都拖延。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 disharmony</td><td>/dɪsˈhɑːrməni/</td><td>★</td><td>★</td><td>名词</td><td>不和谐，不一致</td><td>racial disharmony<br>种族间的不和</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Years of disharmony finally split the once famous band.  多年的不和最终使这支曾经著名的乐队解散。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 dissent</td><td>/dɪˈsent/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★</td><td>不及物动词、名词</td><td>持异议，不同意；反对正统</td><td>voice one's dissent<br>表达异议</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Two judges dissented from the majority opinion of the court.  两位法官对法院的多数意见持异议。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 dissidence</td><td>/ˈdɪsədəns/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>名词</td><td>异议，不同意</td><td>political dissidence<br>政治异见</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The regime punished even mild dissidence among its writers.  该政权连作家中温和的异见也要惩罚。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 disunion</td><td>/dɪsˈjuːnjən/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>名词</td><td>分裂，不和</td><td>the threat of disunion<br>分裂的威胁</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The debate over slavery pushed the nation toward disunion.  关于奴隶制的争论把这个国家推向分裂。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 disunity</td><td>/dɪsˈjuːnəti/</td><td>★</td><td>☆</td><td>名词</td><td>不统一，不和</td><td>disunity within the party<br>党内的不团结</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Open disunity among the leaders cost them the election.  领导层公开的不团结使他们输掉了选举。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 division</td><td>/dɪˈvɪʒən/</td><td>★★★★</td><td>★★☆</td><td>名词</td><td>分歧，不和；分开，部门</td><td>deep division over policy<br>政策上的深刻分歧</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 There is deep division within the committee about the budget.  委员会内部在预算上存在深刻分歧。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 friction</td><td>/ˈfrɪkʃn/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★</td><td>名词</td><td>摩擦；冲突，不和</td><td>friction between them<br>他们之间的摩擦</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Constant friction between the two managers slowed the whole project.  两位经理之间不断的摩擦拖慢了整个项目。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><del>↳近 inharmony</del></td><td>/ɪnˈhɑːrməni/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>名词</td><td>不和谐，不协调</td><td>inharmony among members<br>成员之间不和</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Growing inharmony among members finally split the committee.  成员间日增的不和最终分裂了委员会。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 schism</td><td>/ˈskɪzəm/</td><td>★★</td><td>☆</td><td>名词</td><td>分裂，派系对立</td><td>a bitter schism<br>严重的分裂</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 A bitter schism split the party into two hostile factions.  一场激烈的分裂把该党撕成两个敌对派系。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 strife</td><td>/ˈstraɪf/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>名词</td><td>冲突，争斗，不和</td><td>civil strife<br>内乱</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Years of political strife left the country deeply exhausted.  多年的政治纷争使这个国家极度疲惫。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 accord</td><td>/əˈkɔːrd/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★</td><td>名词、及物动词</td><td>一致，符合；授予</td><td>in accord with<br>与…一致</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 His actions were in accord with the policy he announced.  他的行动与他宣布的政策一致。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 agreement</td><td>/əˈɡriːmənt/</td><td>★★★★</td><td>★★★★</td><td>名词</td><td>一致，同意；协议</td><td>come to an agreement<br>达成协议</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The two firms signed an agreement to share research costs.  两家公司签署协议分担研究费用。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 concord</td><td>/ˈkɑːŋkɔːrd/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★</td><td>名词</td><td>一致，和睦</td><td>live in concord<br>和睦相处</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The treaty brought decades of concord between the two states.  该条约给两国带来数十年的和睦。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 concordance</td><td>/kənˈkɔrdəns/</td><td>★</td><td>☆</td><td>名词</td><td>一致，和谐；索引</td><td>in concordance with the data<br>与数据一致</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The results show close concordance with earlier field studies.  结果显示与早期实地研究高度一致。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 harmony</td><td>/ˈhɑːrməni/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★★</td><td>名词</td><td>和谐，融洽</td><td>live in harmony with<br>与…和谐共处</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The two communities have lived in harmony for generations.  这两个社区世代和睦相处。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 consensus</td><td>/kənˈsensəs/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>名词</td><td>一致同意</td><td>reach a consensus<br>达成一致意见</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The board finally reached a consensus after four hours of debate.  董事会经过四小时辩论终于达成了一致。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 unanimity</td><td>/ˌjuːnəˈnɪməti/</td><td>★☆</td><td>☆</td><td>名词</td><td>全体一致，一致同意</td><td>rare unanimity<br>难得的一致</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 There was surprising unanimity among the usually divided members.  在平时意见分歧的成员中出现了惊人的一致。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><b>dissuade</b></td><td>/dɪˈsweɪd/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>劝阻，劝止</td><td>dissuade him from leaving<br>劝他不要离开</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 His parents tried to dissuade him from leaving school before the final term.  他父母试图劝他不要在最后一学期前退学。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 deter</td><td>/dɪˈtɜːr/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>及物动词</td><td>吓住，威慑</td><td>deter potential criminals<br>威慑潜在罪犯</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Better street lighting may deter thieves from working in this neighborhood.  更好的街道照明或许能威慑小偷不在这一带作案。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 inhibit</td><td>/ɪnˈhɪbɪt/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>抑制，阻碍</td><td>inhibit the growth of bacteria<br>抑制细菌生长</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Cold weather inhibits the growth of these tropical plants.  寒冷天气抑制了这些热带植物的生长。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 encourage</td><td>/ɪnˈkɜːrɪdʒ/</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>及物动词</td><td>鼓励，激励；促进</td><td>encourage students to read<br>鼓励学生阅读</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Good teachers encourage students to ask difficult questions.  好老师鼓励学生提出有难度的问题。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><b>distain</b></td><td>/dɪsˈteɪn/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>及物动词</td><td>贬损，伤害（某人的）名誉</td><td>distain his good name<br>损害他的名誉</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Rumors spread by his rivals distained his good name for many years.  对手散布的谣言多年来一直损害着他的好名声。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 dishonor</td><td>/dɪsˈɑːnər/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★☆</td><td>及物动词、名词</td><td>使丢脸，玷污；不光彩</td><td>dishonor his memory<br>玷污他的名声</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Lying under oath would dishonor everything he had defended.  作伪证会玷污他一生所维护的一切。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 belittle</td><td>/bɪˈlɪtəl/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★☆</td><td>及物动词</td><td>贬低，轻视</td><td>belittle her achievements<br>贬低她的成就</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Don't belittle her achievements just because she works quietly.  别因为她默默工作就贬低她的成就。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 denigrate</td><td>/ˈdenɪɡreɪt/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>诋毁，污蔑</td><td>denigrate a rival<br>诋毁对手</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 He never denigrates his opponents, even after losing badly.  即使惨败，他也从不诋毁对手。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 deprecate</td><td>/ˈdeprəkeɪt/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>及物动词</td><td>不赞成，反对；贬低</td><td>deprecate his efforts<br>贬低他的努力</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 He tends to deprecate his own achievements whenever anyone praises them.  每当有人称赞，他总倾向于贬低自己的成就。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 depreciate</td><td>/dɪˈpriːʃieɪt/</td><td>★</td><td>☆</td><td>及物动词</td><td>贬低…的价值</td><td>depreciate his contribution<br>贬低他的贡献</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Do not depreciate the value of patience in such long negotiations.  不要贬低耐心在这样漫长谈判中的价值。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 degrade</td><td>/dɪˈɡreɪd/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>动词</td><td>贬低，使降级；使退化</td><td>degrade the quality<br>降低质量</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Such insulting jokes degrade everyone who repeats them.  这类侮辱性的笑话贬低了每个转述它们的人。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 praise</td><td>/ˈpreɪz/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>名词、动词</td><td>赞扬，称赞</td><td>win high praise<br>获得高度赞扬</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Her first novel won high praise from serious critics.  她的第一部小说赢得了严肃评论家的高度赞扬。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 acclaim</td><td>/əˈkleɪm/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>名词、及物动词</td><td>称赞</td><td>win critical acclaim<br>赢得评论界赞誉</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Her first novel won wide critical acclaim across Europe and Asia.  她的第一部小说在欧洲和亚洲赢得了广泛的评论界赞誉。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 applaud</td><td>/əˈplɔːd/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>动词</td><td>称赞，赞成；鼓掌</td><td>applaud the decision<br>赞成这一决定</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Teachers applauded the decision to reduce homework for young pupils.  老师们赞成给低年级学生减少作业的决定。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 exalt</td><td>/ɪɡˈzɔːlt/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>赞扬；提升（地位）</td><td>exalt to the skies<br>推崇到极点</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Critics exalt her early novels as masterpieces of the era.  评论家将她早期的小说赞为那个时代的杰作。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 extol</td><td>/ɪkˈstoʊl/</td><td>★</td><td>★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>赞美；吹捧</td><td>extol his virtues<br>赞美他的品德</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Neighbors still extol his kindness during the long winter flood.  邻居们至今仍赞美他在漫长冬季洪水期间的善举。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 glorify</td><td>/ˈɡlɔːrəˌfaɪ/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>赞美，美化</td><td>glorify war and violence<br>美化战争与暴力</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Critics said the film glorified war instead of questioning it.  批评者说这部电影美化战争而非质疑它。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 laud</td><td>/ˈlɔːd/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★☆</td><td>及物动词</td><td>赞美，称赞</td><td>laud his courage<br>赞扬他的勇气</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Critics lauded the young pianist for her fearless interpretation.  评论家赞扬这位年轻钢琴家大胆的演绎。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><b>distal</b></td><td>/ˈdɪstl/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>远离中心的，（神经）末梢的</td><td>the distal end<br>远端</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The surgeon examined the distal end of the broken bone under bright light.  外科医生在强光下检查断骨的远端。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 proximal</td><td>/ˈprɑːksəməl/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>近端的，靠近中心的</td><td>the proximal end<br>近端</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Surgeons repaired the proximal portion of the fractured bone.  外科医生修复了骨折骨头的近端部分。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><del><b>distend</b></del></td><td>/dɪˈstend/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>动词</td><td>（使）膨胀</td><td>distend the stomach<br>使胃膨胀</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Too much gas can distend the stomach and cause considerable discomfort.  气体过多会使胃膨胀，引起相当的不适。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 expand</td><td>/ɪkˈspænd/</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>★★★★</td><td>动词</td><td>扩大，扩张；详述</td><td>expand the business<br>扩大业务</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The company plans to expand into three new markets.  公司计划扩张到三个新市场。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 dilate</td><td>/daɪˈleɪt/</td><td>★</td><td>☆</td><td>动词</td><td>（使）膨胀，扩大</td><td>dilate the pupils<br>使瞳孔扩大</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The doctor used drops to dilate her pupils before examining the retina.  医生先用药水扩大她的瞳孔，再检查视网膜。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 inflate</td><td>/ɪnˈfleɪt/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>动词</td><td>使膨胀；夸大</td><td>inflate the figures<br>夸大数字</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The company inflated its sales figures to impress investors.  公司夸大销售数字以取悦投资者。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 swell</td><td>/ˈswel/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★★★</td><td>动词、名词</td><td>膨胀，增大</td><td>swell in numbers<br>数量增加</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The crowd swelled to thousands by early evening.  傍晚时人群增加到了数千人。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 constrict</td><td>/kənsˈtrɪkt/</td><td>★</td><td>★</td><td>动词</td><td>收缩，压缩；限制</td><td>constrict blood vessels<br>使血管收缩</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Cold air constricts the blood vessels near the skin's surface.  冷空气使皮肤表层的血管收缩。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 compress</td><td>/kəmˈpres/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>动词</td><td>压缩，压紧</td><td>compress the file<br>压缩文件</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 You should compress the file before sending it by email.  用邮件发送之前你应该压缩这个文件。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><b>distill</b></td><td>/dɪˈstɪl/</td><td>★</td><td>★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>蒸馏提纯；提炼出精华</td><td>distill seawater into freshwater<br>蒸馏海水制淡水</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Engineers built a plant to distill seawater into freshwater for the island.  工程师建了一座工厂，把海水蒸馏成淡水供岛上使用。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 purify</td><td>/ˈpjʊrəˌfaɪ/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>净化，使纯净</td><td>purify the water<br>净化水质</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 These tablets purify water so it becomes safe to drink.  这些药片能净化水，使其可安全饮用。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 filter</td><td>/ˈfɪltər/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>动词</td><td>过滤</td><td>filter out impurities<br>滤除杂质</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 This device filters out impurities before the water reaches the tank.  这个装置在水进入水箱前滤除杂质。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 refine</td><td>/rɪˈfaɪn/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>提纯，精炼；改进</td><td>refine crude oil<br>精炼原油</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The plant refines crude oil into gasoline and other useful products.  这家工厂把原油提炼成汽油和其他有用的产品。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 fine</td><td>/ˈfaɪn/</td><td>★★★★☆</td><td>★★★★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>细微的，精细的</td><td>a fine distinction<br>细微的差别</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 He drew a fine distinction between courage and recklessness.  他在勇敢与莽撞之间划出了细微的区别。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><b>distort</b></td><td>/dɪˈstɔːrt/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>扭曲，歪曲</td><td>distort the facts<br>歪曲事实</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Editing the tape that way would distort the facts of the interview.  那样剪辑录音会歪曲采访的事实。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 deform</td><td>/ˌdiːˈfɔːrm/</td><td>★</td><td>★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>使变形，使畸形</td><td>deform the metal frame<br>使金属框变形</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Heat can deform the plastic casing within a few minutes.  高温几分钟内就能使塑料外壳变形。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><del>↳近 misshape</del></td><td>/ˌmɪsˈʃeɪp/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>及物动词</td><td>使变形，使畸形</td><td>misshape the soft clay<br>把软陶弄变形</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Careless packing had misshaped every hat in the box.  粗心的包装把箱里每顶帽子都弄变形了。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 torture</td><td>/ˈtɔːrtʃər/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>及物动词、名词</td><td>折磨，拷打；酷刑</td><td>under torture<br>在酷刑之下</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The long wait for results was pure torture for her.  漫长等待结果对她来说简直是折磨。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 falsify</td><td>/ˈfɔːlsəˌfaɪ/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★☆</td><td>及物动词</td><td>篡改，伪造；证明为假</td><td>falsify the records<br>篡改记录</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 An accountant was accused of falsifying the company's records.  一名会计被指控篡改公司账目。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 misinterpret</td><td>/ˌmɪsɪnˈtɜːrprɪt/</td><td>★☆</td><td>☆</td><td>及物动词</td><td>误解，误读</td><td>misinterpret the data<br>误读数据</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Readers often misinterpret irony as sincere praise.  读者常把讽刺误解为真诚的赞美。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 misrepresent</td><td>/ˌmɪsˌreprɪˈzent/</td><td>★☆</td><td>☆</td><td>及物动词</td><td>误传，篡改</td><td>misrepresent the facts<br>歪曲事实</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Critics accused the report of misrepresenting the facts to favor one side.  批评者指责这份报告歪曲事实以偏向一方。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 pervert</td><td>/pərˈvɜːrt/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>及物动词、名词</td><td>曲解，使颠倒；使堕落</td><td>pervert the course of justice<br>妨碍司法公正</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Lawyers accused him of trying to pervert the truth in court.  律师指控他试图在法庭上曲解真相。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 twist</td><td>/ˈtwɪst/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>动词、名词</td><td>曲解，扭曲；拧</td><td>twist someone's words<br>曲解某人的话</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Do not twist my words to suit your own argument.  不要为了你自己的论点曲解我的话。</td></tr>
</tbody>
</table>

## Unit 10

<table>
<thead><tr>
<th>单词</th>
<th>音标</th>
<th>常用</th>
<th>口语</th>
<th>词性</th>
<th>中文释义</th>
<th>常用短语</th>
</tr></thead>
<tbody>
<tr><td><b>distract</b></td><td>/dɪˈstrækt/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>及物动词</td><td>转移（注意力），使分心</td><td>distract the driver<br>使司机分心</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Loud music can easily distract the driver on an unfamiliar mountain road.  在陌生的山路上，大声的音乐很容易使司机分心。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 abstract</td><td>/æbˈstrækt/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>做总结，概括；使分心</td><td>abstract a long report<br>概括一份长报告</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 She abstracted the key findings into a single page.  她把主要发现概括成一页。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 divert</td><td>/daɪˈvɜːrt/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>使转向；使消遣</td><td>divert attention from<br>转移对…的注意</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The noise diverted my attention from the lecture.  噪音把我的注意力从讲座上引开。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 detract</td><td>/dɪˈtrækt/</td><td>★★</td><td>★</td><td>不及物动词</td><td>贬低，降低价值；使分心</td><td>detract from the value<br>降低其价值</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The ugly frame detracts from the painting.  难看的画框降低了这幅画的价值。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 call off</td><td>/kɔl ˈɔf/</td><td>★★★★☆</td><td>★★★★★</td><td>动词</td><td>取消；叫走，使离开</td><td>call off the search<br>取消搜寻</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 They called off the search because of the storm.  因暴风雨他们取消了搜寻。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 rapt</td><td>/ræpt/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>全神贯注的，入迷的</td><td>rapt attention<br>全神贯注</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The children listened with rapt attention as the old sailor spoke.  老水手讲述时，孩子们全神贯注地听着。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><b>distraught</b></td><td>/dɪˈstrɔːt/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>心烦意乱的，极度不安的</td><td>distraught with grief<br>因悲痛而狂乱</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Distraught with grief, she sat by the window and refused to speak.  她因悲痛而狂乱，坐在窗边不肯说话。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 agitated</td><td>/ˈædʒəˌteɪtɪd/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>焦躁不安的，激动的</td><td>become visibly agitated<br>明显焦躁起来</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 She grew agitated when the police refused to answer her questions.  警察拒绝回答她的问题时，她变得焦躁不安。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 delirious</td><td>/dɪˈlɪriəs/</td><td>★☆</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>神志昏乱的；极度兴奋的</td><td>delirious with fever<br>因发烧而神志不清</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The patient was delirious and kept calling out strange names.  病人神志不清，不停地喊出奇怪的名字。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 distracted</td><td>/dɪsˈtræktəd/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>心烦意乱的，分心的</td><td>look distracted and anxious<br>显得心烦意乱</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 He seemed distracted during the meeting and answered questions vaguely.  会上他显得心不在焉，回答问题含糊其辞。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 frenzied</td><td>/ˈfrenzid/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>狂乱的，疯狂的</td><td>frenzied activity<br>狂乱的活动</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Traders made frenzied attempts to sell before the market closed.  交易员在收市前疯狂地尝试抛售。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 hysterical</td><td>/hɪˈsterɪkəl/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>情绪失控的，歇斯底里的</td><td>become hysterical with grief<br>因悲伤而失控</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The child grew hysterical when she lost sight of her mother.  孩子看不见母亲时变得歇斯底里。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 collected</td><td>/kəˈlektəd/</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>镇定的，冷静的</td><td>calm and collected<br>冷静镇定</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 She stayed cool and collected throughout the tense negotiation.  整个紧张的谈判过程中她都保持冷静镇定。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 composed</td><td>/kəmˈpoʊzd/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>镇静的，沉着的</td><td>remain composed<br>保持镇静</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 She stayed composed while answering the reporters' hostile questions.  她在回答记者敌意的提问时始终保持镇静。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 recollected</td><td>/ˌrekəˈlektɪd/</td><td>★</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>镇定自若的；回忆起的</td><td>a recollected manner<br>从容镇定的态度</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 He faced the hostile crowd with a recollected calm.  他以镇定的从容面对充满敌意的人群。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 self-collected</td><td>/ˌselfkəˈlektɪd/</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>泰然自若的，自我镇定的</td><td>remain self-collected under pressure<br>压力下保持镇定</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The young pilot stayed self-collected as the engine began to fail.  发动机开始故障时，这位年轻飞行员依然泰然自若。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 self-composed</td><td>/ˌselfkəmˈpoʊzd/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>沉着的，自若的</td><td>a self-composed witness<br>沉着的证人</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 She gave a self-composed reply to the reporter's rude question.  她对记者无礼的提问给出了沉着的回答。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 self-possessed</td><td>/ˌselfpəˈzest/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★</td><td>形容词</td><td>冷静沉着的，从容的</td><td>cool and self-possessed<br>冷静自持</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Even at fifteen she was remarkably self-possessed in public.  即便才十五岁，她在公开场合也异常沉着。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><b>diurnal</b></td><td>/daɪˈɜːrnl/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>白天发生或行动的</td><td>diurnal animals<br>昼行动物</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Most birds are diurnal, hunting and singing during daylight hours.  大多数鸟类是昼行的，在白天觅食和鸣叫。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 nocturnal</td><td>/nɑːkˈtɜːrnl/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>夜间的</td><td>nocturnal hunters<br>夜行猎手</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Owls and other nocturnal hunters rely on hearing far more than on sight.  猫头鹰等夜行猎手对听觉的依赖远超视觉。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><b>diverge</b></td><td>/daɪˈvɜːrdʒ/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★☆</td><td>不及物动词</td><td>（使）分叉，散开；分歧</td><td>diverge from the path<br>偏离原有路径</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The two trails diverge just beyond the old wooden bridge.  两条小径在那座老木桥之后就分岔开来。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 branch out</td><td>/bræntʃ ˈaʊt/</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>★★★</td><td>动词</td><td>拓展；扩展业务范围</td><td>branch out into publishing<br>拓展到出版业</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 After years of selling coffee, they branched out into baking.  卖了多年咖啡后，他们拓展到烘焙业。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 divide</td><td>/dɪˈvaɪd/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>动词、名词</td><td>分开，划分；分歧</td><td>divide into groups<br>分成小组</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The teacher divided the class into four groups of five.  老师把班级分成四个五人小组。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 spread</td><td>/spred/</td><td>★★★★</td><td>★★★★</td><td>动词、名词</td><td>传播，散布；蔓延</td><td>spread quickly through town<br>迅速传遍全城</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Rumors spread through the office faster than the memo did.  谣言在办公室传得比通知还快。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 differ</td><td>/ˈdɪfər/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>不及物动词</td><td>不同；意见不一致</td><td>differ in opinion<br>意见不同</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Experts differ widely on how the disease should be treated.  专家们对该疾病的治疗方式看法大相径庭。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 disagree</td><td>/dɪsəˈɡriː/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>不及物动词</td><td>不同意，有分歧</td><td>disagree with the plan<br>不同意这个计划</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Two experts openly disagreed about the cause of the accident.  两位专家对事故原因公开表示分歧。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 converge</td><td>/kənˈvɜːrdʒ/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>动词</td><td>汇集，交汇于一点</td><td>converge on the square<br>汇集到广场</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 All the main roads converge on the square in the center.  所有主干道都在中心广场交汇。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 join</td><td>/ˈdʒɔɪn/</td><td>★★★★</td><td>★★★★</td><td>动词</td><td>连接，接合；加入</td><td>join the two pieces<br>把两块接起来</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Join the ends of the rope with a firm knot.  用牢固的结把绳子两端接起来。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 conform</td><td>/kənˈfɔːrm/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★★</td><td>及物动词、不及物动词</td><td>使协调；遵照，遵从</td><td>conform to the standard<br>符合标准</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 All new buildings must conform to strict safety codes.  所有新建筑都必须符合严格的安全规范。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><b>diversity</b></td><td>/daɪˈvɜːrsəti/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★☆</td><td>名词</td><td>多样性</td><td>a rich diversity of species<br>丰富的物种多样性</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The reef supports a rich diversity of species found nowhere else on earth.  这片珊瑚礁养育着地球上别处没有的丰富物种多样性。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><del>↳近 diverseness</del></td><td>/daɪˈvɜrsnəs/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>名词</td><td>多样性，多样化</td><td>the diverseness of opinion<br>意见的多样性</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The festival celebrates the diverseness of the city's immigrant communities.  这个节日庆祝该市移民社区的多样性。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><del>↳近 multifariousness</del></td><td>/ˌmʌltɪˈferiəsnəs/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>名词</td><td>多样性，形形色色</td><td>the multifariousness of life<br>生活的形形色色</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 His essays capture the multifariousness of modern urban experience.  他的散文捕捉了现代都市体验的多样性。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 multiplicity</td><td>/ˌmʌltəˈplɪsəti/</td><td>★☆</td><td>☆</td><td>名词</td><td>多样性，多重性</td><td>a multiplicity of causes<br>多重原因</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The failure resulted from a multiplicity of small errors.  这次失败源于多种小错误的叠加。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><del>↳近 variousness</del></td><td>/ˈveriəsnəs/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>名词</td><td>多样性，各式各样</td><td>the variousness of nature<br>自然的多样</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Readers admire the variousness of her poetic styles.  读者赞赏她诗歌风格的多样性。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><del>↳近 miscellaneousness</del></td><td>/ˌmɪsəˈleɪniəsnəs/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>名词</td><td>混杂性，五花八门</td><td>the miscellaneousness of the collection<br>藏品的杂多</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The shop's charm lies in the miscellaneousness of its stock.  这家店的魅力在于其存货的五花八门。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><b>divert</b></td><td>/daɪˈvɜːrt/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>使转向；使消遣</td><td>divert attention from<br>转移对…的注意</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The noise diverted my attention from the lecture.  噪音把我的注意力从讲座上引开。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 deflect</td><td>/dɪˈflekt/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>使偏斜，使转向；挡开，避开</td><td>deflect the criticism<br>挡开批评</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The politician deflected every awkward question with a well practiced joke.  那位政客用一个练熟的玩笑挡开每个尴尬的问题。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 redirect</td><td>/ˌriːdəˈrekt/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>使改变方向，重新引导</td><td>redirect the funds<br>转拨资金</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The city redirected traffic away from the flooded underpass.  该市把车流从被淹的地下通道改道。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 swing</td><td>/ˈswɪŋ/</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>动词、名词</td><td>转向，摆动；秋千</td><td>swing sharply to the left<br>猛地转向左侧</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The bus swung left to avoid the fallen branch.  公交车向左急转以避开落下的树枝。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 veer</td><td>/vɪr/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>动词</td><td>（使）转向，（使）改变航线</td><td>veer off course<br>偏离航线</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The old boat began to veer sharply off course in the wind.  那艘旧船在风中开始急剧偏离航线。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 wheel</td><td>/ˈwiːl/</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>动词、名词</td><td>突然转向；轮子</td><td>wheel around suddenly<br>突然转身</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 He wheeled around when he heard footsteps behind him.  他听到身后的脚步声便猛地转身。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><del>↳近 disport</del></td><td>/dɪˈspɔrt/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>动词</td><td>嬉戏，娱乐</td><td>disport oneself on the beach<br>在海滩嬉戏</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Children disported themselves in the shallow water all afternoon.  孩子们整个下午都在浅水里嬉戏。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 entertain</td><td>/ˌentərˈteɪn/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>及物动词</td><td>使娱乐，招待；考虑</td><td>entertain the guests<br>招待客人</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 She entertained us with stories about her years abroad.  她讲述在国外的岁月，让我们听得很开心。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 regale</td><td>/rɪˈɡeɪl/</td><td>★</td><td>★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>使愉悦，款待</td><td>regale friends with stories<br>讲故事逗乐朋友</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The old sailor regaled us with tales of tropical storms.  老水手给我们讲热带风暴的故事，逗得我们很开心。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 solace</td><td>/ˈsɑːləs/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>名词</td><td>安慰</td><td>find solace in music<br>在音乐中找到安慰</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 She found solace in music during the long months of her illness.  在漫长的病中，她在音乐里找到安慰。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 fix</td><td>/ˈfɪks/</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>及物动词、名词</td><td>修理，解决；固定</td><td>fix the leaking pipe<br>修好漏水的管子</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 A neighbor fixed the leaking pipe before the floor was ruined.  邻居在地板毁坏前修好了漏水的管子。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 set</td><td>/ˈset/</td><td>★★★★☆</td><td>★★★★☆</td><td>动词、名词、形容词</td><td>使朝向，安置；设定</td><td>set a new course<br>设定新航向</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The captain set the ship's course toward the northern islands.  船长把船的航向设定为朝北方群岛。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 settle</td><td>/ˈsetəl/</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>★★★★</td><td>动词</td><td>解决，平息；定居</td><td>settle the dispute<br>解决争端</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The two sides finally settled their dispute out of court.  双方最终在庭外解决了争端。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><b>divestiture</b></td><td>/daɪˈvestɪtʃər/</td><td>★</td><td>☆</td><td>名词</td><td>剥夺</td><td>forced divestiture of assets<br>强制剥离资产</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The court ordered forced divestiture of assets in three foreign subsidiaries.  法院下令强制剥离三家海外子公司的资产。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 deprivation</td><td>/ˌdeprɪˈveɪʃn/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>☆</td><td>名词</td><td>剥夺；匮乏</td><td>sleep deprivation<br>睡眠剥夺</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Years of sleep deprivation left the young doctor exhausted and irritable.  多年的睡眠剥夺让这位年轻医生疲惫易怒。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 dispossession</td><td>/ˌdɪspəˈzeʃən/</td><td>★</td><td>☆</td><td>名词</td><td>剥夺（财产），驱逐</td><td>the dispossession of farmers<br>农民被夺去土地</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The war led to the dispossession of thousands of families.  战争导致数千家庭被剥夺财产。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 acquisition</td><td>/ˌækwəˈzɪʃən/</td><td>★★★</td><td>☆</td><td>名词</td><td>获得，收购；获得物</td><td>a major corporate acquisition<br>重大企业收购</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The museum announced the acquisition of three rare manuscripts.  博物馆宣布获得三份珍稀手稿。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><b>divulge</b></td><td>/daɪˈvʌldʒ/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★☆</td><td>及物动词</td><td>泄漏（秘密）</td><td>divulge a secret<br>泄露秘密</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 No employee may divulge a secret learned during the merger negotiations.  任何员工都不得泄露在并购谈判中得知的秘密。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 bare</td><td>/ˈber/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>及物动词、形容词</td><td>揭露，袒露；赤裸的</td><td>bare the truth<br>揭露真相</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 In the interview he bared his family's darkest secrets.  在采访中他揭露了家族最黑暗的秘密。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 disclose</td><td>/dɪsˈkloʊz/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>及物动词</td><td>揭露，公开，透露</td><td>disclose the details<br>公开细节</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The company refused to disclose the details of the settlement.  公司拒绝公开和解协议的细节。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 discover</td><td>/dɪˈskʌvər/</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>及物动词</td><td>发现；揭露</td><td>discover a hidden flaw<br>发现隐藏缺陷</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Auditors discovered that the accounts had been altered.  审计员发现账目被改动过。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 expose</td><td>/ɪkˈspoʊz/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>揭露；使暴露</td><td>expose the fraud<br>揭露欺诈行为</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Two journalists exposed the fraud after months of quiet investigation.  两名记者经过数月暗中调查揭露了这起欺诈。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 uncover</td><td>/ənˈkʌvər/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>及物动词</td><td>揭开，发现，揭露</td><td>uncover the evidence<br>发现证据</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Inspectors uncovered evidence of fraud in the accounting records.  检查人员在账目中发现了欺诈的证据。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 unmask</td><td>/ʌnˈmæsk/</td><td>★</td><td>★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>揭露，揭穿真面目</td><td>unmask the impostor<br>揭穿那个冒充者</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The journalist unmasked the company's secret polluting practices.  记者揭露了这家公司秘密排污的做法。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 unveil</td><td>/ənˈveɪl/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>揭幕，公布，揭示</td><td>unveil a new model<br>发布新款</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The company will unveil a new model at the spring show.  公司将在春季展会上发布新款。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 conceal</td><td>/kənˈsiːl/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>动词</td><td>隐藏，隐瞒</td><td>conceal the truth<br>隐瞒真相</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The witness tried to conceal the truth from the police.  证人试图向警方隐瞒真相。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 cover up</td><td>/ˈkʌvər ˈʌp/</td><td>★★★★</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>动词</td><td>掩盖，遮掩（真相）</td><td>cover up the scandal<br>掩盖丑闻</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Executives tried to cover up the safety violations for years.  高管们多年试图掩盖安全违规。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 hide</td><td>/ˈhaɪd/</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>动词、名词</td><td>隐藏，隐瞒</td><td>hide the evidence<br>藏匿证据</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 She hid the letters in a drawer beneath her sweaters.  她把信藏在毛衣下面的抽屉里。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 mask</td><td>/ˈmæsk/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>及物动词、名词</td><td>掩盖，掩饰；面具</td><td>mask his anger<br>掩饰他的怒气</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Loud music masked the sound of the approaching helicopter.  响亮的音乐掩盖了直升机靠近的声音。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 shroud</td><td>/ʃraʊd/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>及物动词、名词</td><td>将…从视线中隔离，遮蔽</td><td>shrouded in mist<br>笼罩在雾中</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Thick mist shrouded the harbor and hid the ships from view.  浓雾笼罩港口，把船只从视线中遮住。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 veil</td><td>/ˈveɪl/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>及物动词、名词</td><td>遮掩，掩饰；面纱</td><td>veil his true motives<br>掩饰真实动机</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Polite words could not veil his true motives for long.  客气话没能长久掩饰他的真实动机。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><b>dodder</b></td><td>/ˈdɑːdər/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>不及物动词</td><td>蹒跚，颤巍巍地行进</td><td>dodder along the path<br>沿小路蹒跚而行</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The old man doddered along the path with a cane in each hand.  老人两手各持一杖，沿小路蹒跚而行。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 careen</td><td>/kəˈriːn/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>不及物动词</td><td>摇晃疾行，横冲直撞</td><td>careen down the hill<br>摇晃着冲下山坡</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The empty cart careened down the hill and struck a fence.  空车摇晃着冲下山坡，撞上了篱笆。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 lurch</td><td>/lɜːrtʃ/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★☆</td><td>不及物动词</td><td>蹒跚</td><td>lurch to one side<br>向一侧猛地歪倒</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The bus lurched to one side and threw passengers against the windows.  公交车猛地向一侧歪倒，把乘客甩到窗户上。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 teeter</td><td>/ˈtiːtər/</td><td>★</td><td>★</td><td>不及物动词</td><td>摇晃，摇摇欲坠</td><td>teeter on the edge<br>在边缘摇摇欲坠</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The company teetered on the edge of bankruptcy for several months.  公司数月来一直在破产边缘摇摇欲坠。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 totter</td><td>/ˈtɑːtər/</td><td>★</td><td>★</td><td>不及物动词</td><td>摇摇晃晃地走，蹒跚</td><td>totter across the room<br>摇晃着走过房间</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The toddler tottered a few steps and then sat down.  幼儿摇摇晃晃走了几步就坐下了。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 waddle</td><td>/ˈwɑːdl/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★☆</td><td>不及物动词</td><td>摇摇摆摆地走</td><td>waddle like a duck<br>像鸭子般摇摆走</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The fat puppy waddled across the kitchen toward its water bowl.  那只胖胖的小狗摇摇摆摆地穿过厨房走向水碗。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><b>dodge</b></td><td>/dɑːdʒ/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>动词</td><td>躲避</td><td>dodge the question<br>回避问题</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The minister managed to dodge the question three times in one interview.  部长在一次采访中三次成功回避了这个问题。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 avoid</td><td>/əˈvɔɪd/</td><td>★★★★</td><td>★★★★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>避免，回避</td><td>avoid the issue<br>回避问题</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 He carefully avoided answering the reporter's most difficult question.  他小心地避开了记者最难的那个问题。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 escape</td><td>/ɪˈskeɪp/</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>动词、名词</td><td>逃脱，逃避</td><td>escape punishment<br>逃脱惩罚</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Two prisoners escaped through a tunnel under the wall.  两名囚犯从墙下的隧道逃脱。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 shirk</td><td>/ʃɜːrk/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★☆</td><td>动词</td><td>逃避，规避</td><td>shirk responsibility<br>逃避责任</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 No one on this team is allowed to shirk hard work.  这个团队里没人可以逃避苦活。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><del>↳近 malinger</del></td><td>/məˈlɪŋɡər/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>不及物动词</td><td>装病以逃避工作</td><td>malinger to avoid duty<br>装病逃避职责</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Several workers tried to malinger during the busiest week of the harvest season.  有几名工人在收获季最忙的一周里试图装病逃工。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><del>↳近 goldbrick</del></td><td>/ˈɡoʊldbrɪk/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>动词</td><td>逃避工作和责任</td><td>goldbrick on the job<br>上班偷懒摸鱼</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Two workers were fired for goldbricking during the busiest week of the year.  两名工人因在全年最忙的一周偷懒躲活而被解雇。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 evade</td><td>/ɪˈveɪd/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>逃避，规避</td><td>evade the question<br>回避问题</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The witness evaded every question about the missing money.  证人回避了所有关于失踪钱款的问题。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 parry</td><td>/ˈpæri/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>及物动词</td><td>躲避，回避（问题）</td><td>parry the question<br>回避这个问题</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The senator tried to parry every question about his campaign funds.  那位参议员试图回避每一个关于竞选资金的问题。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 sidestep</td><td>/ˈsaɪdstep/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★☆</td><td>及物动词</td><td>回避，避开（问题）</td><td>sidestep the issue<br>回避这个问题</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The candidate neatly sidestepped the issue of rising unemployment.  这位候选人巧妙地回避了失业率上升的问题。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 circumvent</td><td>/ˌsɜːrkəmˈvent/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>躲避，不遵从</td><td>circumvent the rules<br>规避规则</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Some firms hire consultants to circumvent the new tax rules.  一些公司聘请顾问来规避新的税收规则。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 fence</td><td>/ˈfens/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>名词、动词</td><td>栅栏，篱笆；围起</td><td>a wooden fence<br>木栅栏</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 They painted the wooden fence around the garden last summer.  去年夏天他们给花园周围的木栅栏刷了漆。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 hedge</td><td>/hedʒ/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>名词、动词</td><td>树篱，篱笆；用篱笆围住</td><td>a neatly trimmed hedge<br>修剪整齐的树篱</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 A neatly trimmed hedge separated the garden from the road.  一道修剪整齐的树篱把花园与马路隔开。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 avert</td><td>/əˈvɜːrt/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>避免，防止；转移（目光）</td><td>avert a disaster<br>避免灾难</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Quick action by the crew averted a serious accident.  船员的迅速行动避免了一场严重事故。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 elude</td><td>/ɪˈluːd/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★☆</td><td>及物动词</td><td>躲闪，躲避；使无法理解，使困惑</td><td>elude the police<br>躲开警察</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The thief eluded the police for nearly six months before being caught.  小偷躲开警方近六个月才被抓住。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 shun</td><td>/ʃʌn/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>避开，避免</td><td>shun public attention<br>避开公众关注</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The writer chose to shun public attention after winning the prize.  那位作家获奖后选择避开公众关注。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 skirt</td><td>/skɜːrt/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>绕行，避开</td><td>skirt the issue<br>回避问题</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The minister tried to skirt the issue with a long, vague answer.  部长试图用冗长含糊的回答回避这个问题。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 bilk</td><td>/ˈbɪlk/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>及物动词</td><td>骗取，诈骗；逃避（债务）</td><td>bilk investors of millions<br>骗取投资者数百万</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 He bilked elderly clients out of their retirement savings.  他骗走了年长客户的退休储蓄。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 eschew</td><td>/ɪsˈtʃuː/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★☆</td><td>及物动词</td><td>刻意避开；戒绝</td><td>eschew violence<br>戒绝暴力</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The monks eschew luxury and live on whatever the village offers.  僧侣们避开奢华，靠村民所给的一切生活。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 weasel</td><td>/ˈwiːzəl/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>动词、名词</td><td>耍滑逃避（责任）；黄鼠狼</td><td>weasel out of a promise<br>耍滑推掉承诺</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 He tried to weasel out of paying his share.  他试图耍滑不付自己那份钱。</td></tr>
</tbody>
</table>
